The method first estimates a two-dimensional temporal-amplitude density, then applies a Density-Truncated Robust Expectation to limit the influence of distant abnormal points, and finally refines the sequence through an exponential cascade with adaptive stopping. This design aims to improve robustness under out-of-distribution impulse corruptions while keeping the restored trajectory close to the original local structure. Across several benchmark datasets, the proposed method shows consistent gains over classical filters and representative learning-based baselines on curve fidelity, derivative preservation, downstream classification, and runtime efficiency. These results suggest that bounded density-based restoration is a practical option for feature-preserving preprocessing in noisy time-series pipelines.

</details>


### [81] [Signs Beat Floats: Low-Rank Double-Binary Adaptation for On-Device Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24058)
*Yoshihiko Fujisawa,Yuma Ichikawa,Yudai Fujimoto,Akira Sakai,Katsuki Fujisawa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRDBA是一种兼容LoRA的适配器方法，用二进制符号载体和通道级缩放替代低秩因子，显著减小适配器尺寸（超过10倍），在未合并模式下预填充延迟最多增加8%，训练内存约为fp16 LoRA的1.6倍，但在选定场景下匹配fp16 LoRA质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在设备端部署大语言模型时，未合并的LoRA适配器虽然紧凑，但引入了额外的密集浮点分支，增加了存储和计算开销。作者希望进一步压缩适配器，同时保持其质量与可训练/可热插拔的特性。

Method: 将LoRA的两个低秩因子替换为二进制符号载体（仅存储符号），幅度信息通过轻量级的通道级缩放来表示。前向计算变为两次符号累加矩阵乘法与通道级缩放的交替操作。并提供了有限样本分析，说明重建质量由原始LoRA因子的残差与幅度比值决定。

Result: 在未合并适配器模式下，LoRDBA在匹配模型大小时优于低位基线方法，在选定场景下匹配fp16 LoRA质量。适配器大小减少超过10倍（rank=16），预填充延迟最多增加8%，训练内存开销约为fp16 LoRA的1.6倍。

Conclusion: LoRDBA是一种有效的极低比特适配器压缩方案，在显著减小存储和通信开销的同时，保持了可接受的推理延迟和任务质量，适用于设备端大模型适配场景。

Abstract: On-device adaptation of large language models commonly keeps a quantized base model frozen while training and deploying a small, task-specific LoRA adapter. In the unmerged adapter-mode setting, however, the adapter is more than a compact storage module; it introduces an additional dense floating-point branch, maintains a trainable state for local updates, and acts as a unit of communication and hot-swapping.We introduce LoRDBA, a LoRA-compatible adapter that replaces both low-rank factors with binary sign carriers while representing magnitudes through lightweight, channel-wise scales, converting the dense adapter branch into two sign-accumulation matrix multiplications interleaved with channel-wise scaling. A finite-sample analysis shows that reconstruction quality is governed by the residual-to-magnitude ratio of the original LoRA factors. In adapter-mode experiments, LoRDBA outperforms low-bit baselines at matched model sizes while matching fp16 LoRA quality in selected regimes. The unmerged adapter incurs at most 8% prefill latency overhead at matched rank r=16 despite an over 10x reduction in adapter footprint, with moderate training memory overhead of approximately 1.6x that of fp16 LoRA.

</details>


### [82] [Spectral Probe-Circuits: A Three-Step Recipe for Identifying Attention-Head Circuits in Pretrained Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24059)
*Yongzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种三步法识别预训练Transformer中的注意力头电路：基于谱信号的头排序、任务模式筛选、组消融验证。跨多种模型规模（51M-1B活跃/7B总参数）、架构（密集、MoE）和预训练管道验证，发现2-6个头组成的归纳电路在所有模型中因果必要（消融后下降94-100%）。谱信号无需监督即可预测，且可识别特定种子的电路。可识别的专用计算头比例在Pythia系列中保守为17-19%，归纳电路大小为3-11个头（次线性于总头数）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法识别注意力头电路通常依赖有监督标签或梯度归因，缺乏一种通用、无监督且跨架构可迁移的电路识别方法。作者希望提出一种无需标签或归因梯度的、基于信号本身特性的通用电路识别流程。

Method: 三步法：(1) 谱信号——每个注意力头输出的时间积分参与比（participation ratio）作为排序指标，无需标签。(2) 任务模式筛选——从通用排名中筛选出任务特定的候选电路。(3) 组消融——对比匹配随机对照组，完成因果验证。跨8倍参数范围（51M-1B/7B）、两种架构（密集、MoE）和四种预训练管道验证。

Result: (1) 2-6头归纳电路在所有模型中因果必要，消融后合成归纳top-1下降94-100%。(2) 谱信号无监督预测性：在51M探针模型的6个独立种子中，相同计算识别出各种子特定电路。(3) Pythia系列（124M-410M）中可识别的专用计算头比例为17-19%不变，归纳电路大小为3-11个头，次线性于总头数增长。

Conclusion: 提出了一种通用、可迁移的注意力头电路识别方法论，证实归纳电路是所有测试模型中因果必要的核心机制，且专用计算头的比例在模型规模增长中保持守恒。该论文作为三篇论文系列的方法论锚点，后续扩展至预训练发展轨迹和组合任务电路。

Abstract: We present a three-step recipe for identifying attention-head circuits in pretrained transformers. A per-head spectral signal -- the time-integrated participation ratio of each head's attention output -- ranks heads doing sustained content-dependent computation without labels or attribution gradients. A task-pattern screen filters this general indicator into a task-specific candidate circuit, and group ablation against a matched-random control completes the causal claim. We validate across an 8x parameter range (51M to 1B-active / 7B-total), two architecture families (dense, mixture-of-experts), and four pretraining pipelines. The recipe ports: a 2-6 head induction circuit is causally necessary in every model tested, with a 94-100% drop in synthetic-induction top-1 after ablation. The spectral signal is predictive without supervision: on six independent seeds of a 51M-parameter probe model, the same computation identifies the seed-specific circuit on each seed. The fraction of heads doing identifiable specialized computation is conserved at 17-19% across the Pythia family (124M to 410M), while specific induction circuits stay 3-11 heads -- sublinear in total head count. This paper is the methodology anchor of a three-paper program; companion papers extend the recipe to developmental trajectories during pretraining and to composed-task circuits where pattern selectivity decouples from task-causal structure.

</details>


### [83] [Federated Learning over Human-Body Communication for On-Body Edge Intelligence: A Survey, Taxonomy, and BODYFED-HBC Scheduling Vignette](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24062)
*Koffka Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了人体通信（HBC）与联邦学习（FL）在可穿戴体域网中的交叉研究，提出了区分部署场景的分类法，并重点阐述了体信道感知联邦学习（body-channel-aware FL）这一开放问题。文章介绍了BODYFED-HBC参考架构、优化公式与调度算法，以及结合公开数据集和体耦合通信信号损耗模型的可复现仿真方案，最后给出了开放数据集、评估指标、局限性和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前HBC和FL两个领域研究脱节：FL通常抽象了通信层，而HBC研究通常抽象了学习和模型更新流量。本文旨在弥合这一鸿沟，提出HBC与FL在可穿戴设备中的系统化交叉研究框架。

Method: 通过系统性综述，提出分类法（区分体内、体枢纽、跨用户和临床云四种FL部署场景），并构建BODYFED-HBC参考架构，包含优化公式、调度算法及可复现的仿真设计（公开数据集+经验性体耦合通信信号损耗模型）。

Result: 识别出体信道感知FL这一开放问题——即学习协议的客户端选择、更新压缩和聚合过程需由姿态相关的HBC链路、剩余能量、传感器内存和隐私风险共同控制。提供了BODYFED-HBC参考架构、优化公式和调度算法，以及可复现的仿真设计。

Conclusion: 总结了开放数据集、评估指标、研究局限性，并为硬件层以上的计算机科学研究者指出了未来研究方向。

Abstract: Human-body communication (HBC) is a promising physical substrate for wearable body-area networks because it can localize communication around the body and reduce the burden of conventional radio links. Federated learning (FL) is a promising learning substrate because it can reduce raw-data centralization for physiological and behavioral sensing. Yet these two literatures remain weakly connected: FL for wearables usually abstracts the communication layer, whereas HBC research usually abstracts learning and model-update traffic. This article surveys the intersection of HBC, wireless body-area networks, wearable FL, Internet-of-Bodies privacy, and edge-intelligence optimization. We propose a taxonomy that distinguishes intra-body, body-hub, cross-user, and clinical-cloud FL deployments, and we identify the open problem of body-channel-aware FL: learning protocols whose client selection, update compression, and aggregation are controlled by posture-dependent HBC links, residual energy, sensor memory, and privacy risk. To make the research agenda concrete, we introduce BODYFED-HBC as a reference architecture and provide an optimization formulation and scheduling algorithm. We further specify a reproducible simulation vignette that combines public wearable datasets with empirical body-coupled-communication signal-loss models. The article concludes with open datasets, evaluation metrics, limitations, and research directions for computer scientists working above the hardware layer.

</details>


### [84] [Generative Representation Learning on Hyper-relational Knowledge Graphs via Masked Discrete Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24064)
*Jaejun Lee,Seheon Kim,Joyce Jiyoung Whang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出超关系知识图谱（HKG）上的新任务——事实生成（fact generation），即从任意掩码查询中生成有效超关系事实，并提出了首个生成式表示学习方法KREPE，通过掩码离散扩散建模局部事实组件与全局结构的概率分布，统一了链接预测与事实生成，在标准HKG链接预测基准上达到SOTA，并优于基于LLM的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HKG推理方法局限于简单链接预测（仅缺失单个实体/关系），无法处理现实场景中多个甚至全部成分同时缺失的情况。本文旨在填补这一空白，引入更具一般性的事实生成任务。

Method: 提出KREPE，一种基于掩码离散扩散的生成式表示学习方法。通过上下文消息传递（contextual message passing）建模事实内依赖关系，通过聚合随机采样上下文（stochastically sampled contexts）建模事实间相关性，学习缺失成分关于局部事实组件和全局结构的条件概率分布。

Result: 在标准HKG链接预测基准上达到SOTA性能；在生成新颖且正确事实方面优于基于LLM的基线方法；统一了链接预测与事实生成任务。

Conclusion: KREPE通过生成式框架成功将HKG推理从受限的链接预测扩展至更灵活的事实生成，证明了离散扩散模型在超关系知识图谱上的有效性。

Abstract: Hyper-relational knowledge graphs (HKGs) effectively represent complex facts. While inferring new knowledge in HKGs is a critical problem, current methods cast it as a simple link prediction, assuming that nearly all entities and relations within a fact are known, leaving only a single blank to be filled. However, this restricted assumption may not hold in real-world scenarios in which multiple, or even all, constituent components of a fact may be missing simultaneously. To bridge this gap, we introduce a task called fact generation: generating a valid hyper-relational fact from an arbitrarily masked query, i.e., completing a partially observed fact or generating a fact from scratch. We propose KREPE, the first generative representation learning method for HKGs that learns to model the probability distributions of missing components conditioned on the local fact components and global structure of HKGs via a masked discrete diffusion. KREPE models both the intra-fact dependencies by contextual message passing and inter-fact correlations by aggregating stochastically sampled contexts. KREPE seamlessly unifies link prediction and fact generation within a single training framework, achieving state-of-the-art performance on standard HKG link prediction benchmarks and outperforming LLM-based baselines in generating novel and correct facts.

</details>


### [85] [Riemannian Archetypal Analysis: Interpretable non-linear data analysis on deformed star distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24113)
*Willem Diepeveen,Deanna Needell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数据驱动拉回几何的黎曼原型分析（RAM）方法，通过变形星形分布与凸松弛优化，在保持经典原型分析可解释性的同时，实现非线性数据的几何感知降维、去噪与分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典原型分析具有可解释性，但其线性几何在处理强非线性数据时性能受限；现有神经扩展方法虽提升了灵活性，却往往削弱了原型和插值的几何意义。本文旨在结合经典方法的可解释性与现代非线性模型的表达能力。

Method: 提出基于数据驱动拉回几何的黎曼原型分析：引入变形星形分布及相关拉回黎曼几何；定义黎曼原型映射（RAM）为向原型测地凸组合流形的投影；提出先凸松弛后非凸精化的优化方案；设计从数据中学习变形星形分布的方案。

Result: 在合成数据和MNIST上的实验表明，该框架能产生有意义的测地线、有效的去噪投影以及几何感知的分类，同时揭示了当前优化的局限性。

Conclusion: RAM成功结合线性原型分析的可解释性与非线性模型的表达能力，为几何感知的数据分析提供了新范式，但优化方面仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Classical archetypal analysis is appealing for its interpretability, but its linear geometry can limit performance on data with strongly non-linear structure; at the same time, existing neural extensions improve flexibility while often weakening the geometric meaning of archetypes and interpolations. In this work, we develop a Riemannian version of archetypal analysis based on data-driven pullback geometry for real-valued data, with the goal of combining the interpretability of classical archetypal analysis with the expressive power of modern non-linear models. We introduce a class of deformed star distributions together with associated pullback Riemannian geometry to provide a statistical interpretation of the resulting manifold mappings, define the Riemannian archetypal mapping (RAM) as a projection onto the manifold of geodesically convex combinations of archetypes, and propose a practical optimization scheme based on convex relaxation followed by non-convex refinement. We further propose a learning scheme that yields reasonable, albeit generally suboptimal, deformed star distributions from data. Experiments on synthetic examples and MNIST show that the resulting framework produces meaningful geodesics, useful denoising projections, and geometry-aware classifications, while also clarifying where current optimization limitations remain.

</details>


### [86] [Knowledge Graph Modulated Deep Learning for Limited-Sample Clinical Data Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24162)
*Yuwei Xue,Sakib Mostafa,James Zou,Joseph Liao,Maximilian Diehn,Ash A. Alizadeh,Lei Xing,Md. Tauhidul Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Graph-in-Graph (GiG)框架，将患者数据表示为以生物知识图谱定义边、患者特异性测量定义节点特征的模块化图，通过保留基因-基因交互和通路拓扑结构实现数据高效的临床预测。在近9700名患者、五项临床任务中，GiG一致优于传统和SOTA方法，在小样本场景下提升尤为显著（前列腺癌诊断任务中macro-F1提升高达49个百分点），且性能提升源于知识图谱的生物学结构而非单纯的图建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物医学AI模型无法直接利用图结构化的生物知识，需压缩为低维表示导致结构信息丢失，尤其在有限样本临床研究中性能下降。亟需一种能保持生物知识图谱结构完整性的深度学习框架。

Method: GiG将每位患者构建为独立模块化图：节点特征来自患者特异性测量（如基因表达），边由 curated 生物知识图谱（如通路图）定义，允许同时整合多个知识图谱并保留基因-基因交互和通路拓扑结构，在患者级表示学习中保持结构完整性。

Result: 在近9700名患者、五项临床任务（包括液体活检癌症检测、前列腺癌诊断、32类泛癌分类）中，GiG一致优于传统和SOTA方法。前列腺癌诊断任务中macro-F1提升高达49个百分点。对照实验用随机拓扑替换真实通路图证实性能提升源于生物学知识图谱结构。

Conclusion: 知识图谱调制深度学习可提高临床数据分析的鲁棒性、可解释性和样本效率，为将生物知识图谱整合到预测模型提供了原则性框架。

Abstract: Biological systems are governed by structured molecular interactions, where pathways, regulatory circuits, and functional gene relationships shape cellular behavior and disease progression. Much of this knowledge is naturally represented as graphs. However, most biomedical AI models cannot directly use graph-encoded biological knowledge and instead require compressed low-dimensional representations, which can lose important structure and reduce performance, especially in limited-sample clinical studies. Here, we introduce Graph-in-Graph (GiG), a knowledge graph-modulated deep learning framework for data-efficient clinical prediction. GiG represents each patient as a standalone modular graph, in which curated biological knowledge graphs define edges and patient-specific measurements, such as gene expression, define node features. This design allows multiple biological knowledge graphs to be integrated while preserving gene-gene interactions and pathway topology during patient-level representation learning. Across cohorts comprising nearly 9,700 patients and five clinical tasks, including liquid biopsy cancer detection, prostate cancer diagnosis, and 32-class pan-cancer classification, GiG consistently outperforms traditional and state-of-the-art methods, with the largest gains in limited-sample settings. On the challenging prostate cancer diagnosis task, GiG improves macro-F1 by up to 49 percentage points relative to competing methods. Control experiments replacing real pathway graphs with random topologies confirm that these gains arise from biologically grounded knowledge graph structure rather than graph modeling alone. These findings show that knowledge graph-modulated deep learning can improve robustness, interpretability, and sample efficiency in clinical data analysis, and provide a principled framework for integrating biological knowledge graphs into predictive modeling.

</details>


### [87] [PromptAudit: Auditing Prompt Sensitivity in LLM-Based Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24171)
*Steffen J. Camarato,Yahya Hmaiti,Mandana Ghadamian,David Mohaisen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PromptAudit框架，系统评估不同提示策略对LLM漏洞检测性能的影响。在5个模型、6074个代码样本上测试发现：标准思维链整体最优，少样本提示对敏感模型有益，自适应思维链抑制召回率，自一致性导致过度弃权。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在漏洞检测中越来越常用，但不同提示策略对其可靠性的影响尚未被系统刻画。现有研究缺乏在控制其他变量条件下隔离提示效应的评估框架。

Method: 提出PromptAudit控制评估框架，固定数据集、解码和解析环节，仅变化提示策略。在5个开源权重模型上，使用5种提示策略对6074个代码样本（1000个CVE，16种编程语言）进行评估，测量准确率、召回率、弃权率、覆盖率和有效F1。

Result: 标准思维链提示实现最强整体操作性能；少样本提示对提示敏感模型有显著收益；自适应思维链频繁抑制召回率；自一致性导致过度弃权，大幅降低有效性能。

Conclusion: 漏洞检测行为由模型和提示共同决定，提示敏感性是系统级首要属性，必须在评估和部署中明确刻画。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for vulnerability detection, yet their reliability under different prompt formulations remains uncharacterized. We present PromptAudit, a controlled evaluation framework that isolates prompt effects by fixing the dataset, decoding, and parsing while varying only the prompting strategy. Using five prompting strategies across five open-weight models on 1,000 CVEs (6,074 code samples spanning 16 programming languages), we evaluate accuracy, recall, abstention, coverage, and effective F1. We find that standard chain-of-thought prompting achieves the strongest overall operational performance, while few-shot prompting provides model-dependent benefits that are most pronounced for prompt-sensitive models. In contrast, adaptive chain-of-thought frequently suppresses recall and self-consistency induces excessive abstention, sharply reducing effective performance. These results show that vulnerability detection behavior is jointly determined by the model and the prompt, and that prompt sensitivity is a first-class system property that must be explicitly characterized in evaluation and deployment.

</details>


### [88] [Characterizing the Representational Capacity of Neural Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24210)
*Robin Young*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文严格证明了神经过程（NP）各架构的表示能力存在层级关系：CNP只能表示依赖有限期望特征的函数，ANP通过查询依赖重加权严格泛化CNP，ConvCNP与ANP不可比，TNP通过自注意力层数控制上下文交互范围，潜变量NP的生成能力受限于编码器且需潜在维度随上下文规模增长才能匹配高斯过程后验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分析神经过程各变体架构（CNP、ANP、ConvCNP、TNP及潜变量变体）的表示能力，厘清它们能表示哪些函数、不能表示哪些函数，为基于任务结构选择合适架构提供理论基础。

Method: 通过理论证明，分别分析CNP、ANP、ConvCNP、TNP及潜变量NP的表示容量，建立严格的架构层级关系，并比较各架构与高斯过程后验的匹配能力。

Result: 1) CNP只能表示依赖上下文分布有限个期望特征的函数；2) ANP通过查询依赖重加权严格泛化CNP，可实现核平滑器；3) ConvCNP与ANP不可比，区别在于平稳性与平移等变性；4) TNP的L层自注意力可捕获L跳上下文交互；5) 有限维潜变量NP能生成连贯样本但无法规避编码器局限，需潜在维度随上下文规模线性增长才能匹配GP后验。

Conclusion: 建立了NP架构的严格表示能力层级，为根据任务结构（如是否需要核平滑、平稳性、交互范围等）选择合适NP架构提供了理论依据。

Abstract: What functions can Neural Processes represent? We analyze the representational capacity of popular NP architectures: Conditional Neural Processes (CNPs), Attentive Neural Processes (ANPs), Transformer Neural Processes (TNPs), and their latent variants. We prove these architectures form a strict hierarchy. CNP-representable functions are exactly those depending on finitely many expected features of the context distribution. ANPs strictly generalize CNPs via query-dependent reweighting, enabling kernel smoothers. ConvCNPs and ANPs are incomparable; each contains functions outside the other, separated by stationarity versus translation equivariance. TNPs with $L$ self-attention layers capture $L$-hop context interactions. For latent NPs, we show finite-dimensional latents provide coherent sampling but do not circumvent encoder limitations; matching GP posterior distributions requires latent dimension scaling with context size. These results provide a theoretical foundation for architecture selection based on task structure.

</details>


### [89] [PrivFusion: A Privacy-preserving Multi-Agent Framework for Harmonizing Distributed Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24249)
*Anisa Halimi,Liubov Nedoshivina,Kieran Fraser,Stefano Braghin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PrivFusion是一个隐私保护的多智能体框架，可自动完成结构化数据集在联邦学习前的协调（数据对齐），有效减少人工干预，并在四个异构COVID-19数据集上验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽能解决数据隐私问题，但多机构数据集的异质性（特征差异）严重阻碍其应用，而数据协调这一关键前提步骤常被忽视，缺乏自动化、隐私保护的解决方案。

Method: 提出PrivFusion框架，使用多个智能体分别分析本地数据，跨站点聚类语义相似的特征，并通过迭代推荐变换方案直至各站点数据对齐，全程保持隐私保护。

Result: 在四个异构COVID-19数据集上的评估表明，PrivFusion能够高效、有效地完成多站点数据协调，同时大幅降低人工工作量。

Conclusion: PrivFusion为联邦学习中关键的数据协调问题提供了一种自动化的隐私保护解决方案，能够显著提升多站点异构数据场景下的联邦训练可行性。

Abstract: The growing availability of clinical data has increased the use of machine learning, yet centralized data aggregation is often infeasible for sensitive health information. Federated Learning (FL) offers a distributed alternative, but its adoption is limited by substantial heterogeneity across institutional datasets, making harmonization a critical but frequently overlooked prerequisite for multi-site analytics. We introduce PrivFusion, a privacy-preserving multi-agent framework that automates the harmonization of structured datasets prior to federated training. PrivFusion uses agents to analyze local data, cluster semantically similar features across sites, and provide iterative transformation recommendations until alignment is achieved. Evaluation across four heterogeneous COVID-19 datasets demonstrates that PrivFusion effectively and efficiently harmonizes multi-site data while substantially reducing manual effort.

</details>


### [90] [Optimizing Digital Therapeutic Interventions: Online Learning under Endogenous Adherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24261)
*Eric Pulick,Stephanie Carpenter,Matthew Buman,Yonatan Mintz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数字疗法（DT）决策支持框架，通过线性动力系统（LDS）建模患者对治疗建议的参与能力和依从性行为，并设计了基于乐观主义的UCB-BOLD算法实现在线治疗选择。实验表明，该框架在考虑推荐效应和依从效应的交互影响下，相比基准方法可将条件风险价值遗憾降低2-3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字疗法决策支持框架要么只建模治疗建议对依从性的影响，要么将依从性视为外生变量，忽略了治疗建议和过去依从性共同影响未来依从性的内生关系，导致在长期慢性病干预中资源分配效率低下。

Method: 1) 使用线性动力系统（LDS）建模患者随时间变化的参与能力，通过logit连接函数将潜变量与依从行为内生连接；2) 建立了该模型的有限时间辨识保证，扩展了经典LDS理论；3) 提出基于乐观主义的UCB-BOLD算法进行在线治疗选择，并证明其具有亚线性遗憾。

Result: 在基于微随机试验数据生成的合成患者队列上，UCB-BOLD相比最优基准方法将条件风险价值遗憾降低了2-3倍；消融实验表明，虽然短视或启发式方法对部分患者类型足够，但显式规划推荐效应和依从效应对其他患者类型收益显著。

Conclusion: 数字疗法决策支持工具应纳入动力系统模型，通过对推荐效应和依从效应的联合建模与规划，能够在有限信息和资源下更有效地改善患者长期健康结局。

Abstract: A critical challenge facing clinicians managing chronic disease interventions is sustaining long-run patient health given limited information and resources. Digital therapeutics (DTs) provide a cost-effective way to manage interventions at scale through repeated interactions (e.g. daily treatment recommendations), but patient success is highly dependent on their adherence. Behavioral psychology suggests that both treatment recommendations and past adherence affect future adherence, yet existing decision support frameworks for DTs model only recommendation effects or treat adherence as exogenous context, leaving a key gap in model and algorithm development. To address this gap, we present a DT decision support framework that captures both recommendation and adherence effects, allowing clinicians to better plan treatment recommendations. We model a patient's time-varying capacity for engagement with treatment using a linear dynamical system (LDS) that captures both recommendation and adherence effects, endogenously connected to adherence behavior with a logit link. We establish finite-time identification guarantees for this model, extending LDS results to our setting. Next, we propose an optimism-based algorithm, UCB-BOLD, for online treatment selection and prove that it achieves sublinear regret. We evaluate UCB-BOLD against benchmarks via ablation studies on a synthetic patient cohort generated using micro-randomized trial data. DT decision support tools can include dynamical models to enable decision makers to efficiently use the data in DT settings to improve patient health through effective resource allocation. While myopic or heuristic approaches suffice for some patient types, the benefits of explicitly planning around recommendation and adherence effects are significant for others; UCB-BOLD achieves 2-3x lower conditional value-at-risk regret than the next-best benchmark.

</details>


### [91] [Faithfulness as Information Flow: Evaluating and Training Faithful Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24286)
*Jinghan Jia,Joe Benton,Eric Easley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究思维链(CoT)推理的忠实性问题，提出基于信息流视角的忠实性评估框架（充分性、完备性、必要性），并引入基于熵、KL散度和梯度的诊断指标。在此基础上，提出了面向验证器的在线RL更新时干预方法（注意力掩码、反向梯度掩码、CoT梯度、对抗扰动），在提示算术、可破解代码修复和DAPO-Math等场景下有效增强CoT中介性，降低捷径依赖和奖励破解行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型在CoT推理中可能利用"提示到答案"的捷径绕过CoT，导致可见的推理轨迹具有误导性。为了实现对语言模型的可监控推理，需要确保CoT推理忠实反映实际计算过程，因此需要系统性方法评估和提升CoT的忠实性。

Method: 1) 提出基于信息流的结构化视角，定义忠实推理的三项互补属性：充分性（CoT包含足够信息）、完备性（答案仅依赖CoT信息）、必要性（CoT信息对答案是必需的）；2) 分别用熵、掩码KL散度和梯度方法实例化上述诊断指标；3) 提出更新时干预方法（注意力掩码、反向梯度掩码、CoT梯度、对抗扰动）用于验证器基于策略的在线强化学习训练。

Result: 干预方法在提示算术、可破解代码修复和DAPO-Math（错误提示注入）场景下，使行为和结构指标向更强的CoT中介性偏移；使捷径和奖励破解行为在CoT中更透明；改善了任务无关的忠实性指标；在某些设置下降低了对错误提示的敏感性。同时发现了KL散度诊断的低熵失效模式，梯度方法更为稳定。

Conclusion: 训练过程中控制信息流是提升CoT推理忠实性和可监控性的实用路径。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is useful for monitoring language models only when the reasoning trace faithfully reflects the computation that produces the final answer. However, models can rely on prompt-to-answer shortcuts that bypass the CoT, making the visible reasoning trace misleading even when it appears plausible. We study CoT faithfulness through a structural information-flow perspective: faithful reasoning should route answer-relevant information through the mediated path from prompt to CoT to answer, rather than through a direct prompt-to-answer shortcut. This perspective yields a task-agnostic framework based on three complementary properties, sufficiency, completeness, and necessity, which we instantiate with entropy-based, masked-KL, and gradient-based diagnostics. We show that these metrics recover externally judged faithfulness differences in hinted reasoning, and identify a low-entropy failure mode of KL-based diagnostics where gradient-based measures remain more stable. Building on this analysis, we introduce update-time interventions for verifier-based on-policy RL, including attention masking, backward-only gradient masking, CoT gradients, and adversarial perturbations of prompt representations. Across hinted arithmetic, reward-hackable code repair, and DAPO-Math models trained without hints but evaluated under wrong-hint injection, our interventions shift behavioral and structural indicators toward stronger CoT mediation. In particular, they make shortcut and reward-hacking behavior more transparent in the CoT and improve task-agnostic faithfulness metrics, while in some settings also reducing wrong-hint susceptibility. Our results suggest that controlling information flow during training is a practical route toward more faithful and monitorable CoT reasoning. Code is available at https://github.com/safety-research/faithful-cot.

</details>


### [92] [TUBE: Tangent Upper Bound on Evidence for Discrete Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24292)
*Arseny Ivanov,Sergei Kholkin,Vladislav Gromadskii,Grigoriy Ksenofontov,Ivan Oseledets,Alexander Korotin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TUBE（证据正切上界），一种可对对数似然进行无偏蒙特卡洛估计的变分上界，用于评估离散扩散模型等生成模型。通过TUBE评估发现，块状掩码扩散模型和块状任意顺序自回归模型的似然性能严格低于精确的自回归模型基线，表明自回归模型在似然方面仍占主导地位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离散扩散模型无法精确计算对数似然这一标准评估指标，现有工作依赖证据下界（ELBO），无法得知真实似然值可能高出多少，因此需要一种能够提供上界的方法来准确评估模型性能。

Method: 提出了TUBE（Tangent Upper Bound on Evidence），一种对数似然的变分上界，支持无偏蒙特卡洛估计。该方法可推广至多种潜变量模型，包括掩码扩散模型（MDMs）、任意顺序自回归模型（AO-ARMs）及其块状变体。

Result: 将TUBE应用于块状MDMs和块状AO-ARMs，发现这些模型的似然严格低于精确的自回归模型基线，表明自回归模型在似然评估中仍占主导地位。

Conclusion: TUBE为评估离散扩散模型提供了有效的上界工具，实证结果表明当前离散扩散模型在似然上尚未超越自回归模型。

Abstract: Log-likelihood is a standard metric for evaluating generative models. Unfortunately, in contrast to autoregressive models (ARMs), discrete diffusion models generally do not admit exact computation of this quantity. Existing evaluations, therefore, rely on the evidence lower bound (ELBO), leaving unclear how much higher the true value may be. We address this by introducing the Tangent Upper Bound on Evidence (TUBE), a variational upper bound on log-likelihood that admits an unbiased Monte Carlo estimator. Our TUBE extends across latent-variable models, including masked diffusion models (MDMs), any-order ARMs (AO-ARMs), and block variants of both. Applied to block MDMs and block AO-ARMs, TUBE reveals our key empirical finding that these models lie strictly below the exact ARM baseline, showing that ARMs still dominate in likelihood.

</details>


### [93] [Efficient DP-SGD for LLMs with Randomized Clipping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24879)
*Enayat Ullah,Sai Aparna Aketi,Devansh Gupta,Huanyu Zhang,Meisam Razaviyayn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DP-SGD-RC，一种带有随机裁剪的差分隐私随机梯度下降变体，利用Hutchinson和Hutch++随机迹估计方法降低每样本梯度范数估计的内存开销，在保持隐私保证和模型效用的情况下显著减少内存和计算复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DP训练实现依赖快速梯度裁剪技术，其内存开销为O(B min{T², d²})，随着模型规模和上下文长度增长变得难以承受，亟需降低内存和计算复杂度的隐私训练方法。

Method: 提出DP-SGD-RC，采用随机裁剪替代确定性裁剪，利用Hutchinson估计器及其改进变体Hutch++进行随机迹估计，以降低每样本梯度范数估计的内存开销，并提供严格的隐私分析证明其噪声倍率可与确定性裁剪相竞争。

Result: 在Llama 3.2-1B模型上对长上下文基准（包括分类、问答和摘要任务）进行微调实验，DP-SGD-RC在匹配基线效用的同时显著减少了内存和计算需求。

Conclusion: DP-SGD-RC通过随机裁剪和随机迹估计有效解决了DP训练中大模型和长上下文场景下的内存瓶颈问题，为实际部署差分隐私LLM训练提供了更高效的方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are trained on vast datasets that may contain sensitive information. Differential privacy (DP), the de facto standard for formal privacy guarantees, provides a principled framework for training LLMs with provable privacy protection. However, state-of-the-art DP training implementations rely on fast gradient clipping techniques with memory overhead $O(B \min\{T^2, d^2\})$, where $B$ is the batch size, $T$ is the sequence length, and $d$ is the model width. This becomes prohibitive as both model size and context length grow. We propose DP-SGD-RC, a novel variant of DP-SGD with randomized clipping that reduces memory and compute complexity. DP-SGD-RC leverages stochastic trace estimation methods, specifically Hutchinson's estimator[Hutchinson, 1989] and its improved variant, Hutch++[Meyer et al., 2021], to reduce the memory footprint of per-sample gradient norm estimation. We provide a tight privacy analysis showing that DP-SGD-RC achieves noise multipliers competitive with deterministic clipping. Experiments fine-tuning Llama~3.2-1B on long-context benchmarks spanning classification, question answering, and summarization tasks demonstrate that DP-SGD-RC matches baseline utility while significantly reducing memory and compute requirements.

</details>


### [94] [Private Adaptive Covariance Estimation via Gaussian Graphical Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24295)
*Cecilia Ferrando,Miguel Fuentes,Brett Mullins,Cameron Musco,Daniel Sheldon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PACE-GGM 是一种自适应差分隐私协方差估计方法，通过将隐私预算集中用于最有信息量的经验协方差矩阵条目，并在每次迭代中测量被选中的条目后利用最大熵重建目标重建完整协方差矩阵，在高维和低到中等隐私预算场景下持续优于传统高斯机制等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有差分隐私协方差估计方法（如高斯机制）对所有条目均匀添加噪声，导致在变量边界不同的自然场景下噪声过大。本文旨在设计一种数据自适应的隐私预算分配策略，将有限的隐私预算集中在信息量最大的条目上，从而提高高维和低到中等隐私预算下的估计精度。

Method: PACE-GGM 迭代执行以下步骤：1) 选择当前重建误差最大的协方差矩阵条目；2) 对该条目使用高斯机制进行差分隐私测量；3) 基于最大熵重建目标，利用已测量条目重建完整的协方差矩阵，从而自然地诱导出高斯图模型结构。该方法利用各变量可独立提供边界的事实，实现条目级别的差异化噪声注入。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，PACE-GGM 在估计误差方面一致优于传统高斯机制及其他基线方法，尤其在高维和低到中等隐私预算（低噪声）场景下提升最为显著。

Conclusion: PACE-GGM 通过自适应隐私预算分配有效解决了差分隐私协方差估计中噪声均匀分布导致的高维性能退化问题，在实用隐私预算范围内取得了更准确的估计结果，为差分隐私下的高维统计估计提供了新的有效范式。

Abstract: We propose PACE-GGM, a data-adaptive differentially private method for covariance estimation that concentrates its privacy budget on the most informative entries of the empirical covariance matrix, rather than perturbing all entries. This applies in the natural setting where the modeler supplies separate bounds for each variable, so that individual entries can be measured with less noise than the full matrix. In each round, our method selects a poorly approximated entry, measures it using the Gaussian mechanism, and then reconstructs a full covariance matrix using a maximum-entropy reconstruction objective, leading to a Gaussian graphical model structure. Experiments on diverse real-world datasets demonstrate consistent improvements in estimation error with respect to the Gaussian mechanism and other baselines, particularly in high-dimensional and low-to-moderate privacy regimes.

</details>


### [95] [Global linear convergence of entropy-regularized softmax policy gradient beyond tabular MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24939)
*Ziyue Chen,David Šiška,Lukasz Szpruch*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续状态-动作空间、无限时域熵正则化MDP的策略梯度全局收敛性。在log-linear softmax策略和线性函数逼近下，建立了非均匀Polyak--Łojasiewicz不等式，并识别了两种特征机制（全仿射跨度特征和单纯形值特征），证明了正则化目标沿梯度流的全局线性收敛（$\mathcal{O}(e^{-Ct})$），将表格设置下的理论扩展到了连续空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将熵正则化softmax策略梯度的全局收敛理论从表格设置扩展到连续状态-动作空间，解决线性函数逼近下策略梯度方法的收敛性难题。

Method: 采用log-linear softmax策略与线性函数逼近，建立非均匀PŁ不等式，并通过两种特征机制（全仿射跨度特征和单纯形值特征）对非均匀常数进行边界控制，分析Fisher信息矩阵或非中心特征协方差矩阵的最小特征值下界。

Result: 证明了正则化目标沿梯度流全局线性收敛，次优性以$\mathcal{O}(e^{-Ct})$速率衰减。

Conclusion: 成功将熵正则化softmax策略梯度的全局收敛理论从表格设置扩展到连续状态-动作空间，为线性函数逼近下的策略优化提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study the global convergence of policy gradient for infinite-horizon entropy-regularized Markov decision processes (MDPs) with continuous state and action spaces. We consider log-linear softmax policies with linear function approximation, which extend the tabular softmax parameterization while retaining a tractable policy class. Under $Q^π_τ$-realizability for the regularized state-action value function, we first establish a non-uniform Polyak--Łojasiewicz (PŁ) inequality. The non-uniformity arises through degeneracy of constants associated with the policy geometry, namely the Fisher information matrix or an uncentered feature covariance matrix. We then identify two feature regimes under which this non-uniform constant can be bounded along the gradient flow. For full-affine-span features, we prove radial unboundedness of the KL regularizer and show that the smallest eigenvalue of the Fisher information matrix remains bounded below by an initialization-dependent positive constant. For simplex-valued features, we prove an analogous radial unboundedness result in the subspace orthogonal to the all-ones vector and obtain a uniform lower bound for the smallest eigenvalue of the uncentered covariance matrix. These results imply global linear convergence of the regularized objective along the gradient flow, i.e. suboptimality decaying as $\mathcal{O}(e^{-Ct})$ for some $C>0$. Our analysis extends the global convergence theory of entropy-regularized softmax policy gradient beyond the tabular setting of Agarwal et al. (2020); Bhandari and Russo (2024); Mei et al. (2020).

</details>


### [96] [LLMs Show No Signs Of Individuated Metacognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24299)
*M. Moran,Mark Whiting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对20个前沿大语言模型在六个基准上的二元置信度判断进行分解分析，发现模型间置信度矩阵近似秩一结构，单一主导因子解释了大部分潜在方差。去除所有模型一致认同的项目后，置信度与性能之间的关系基本消失。数学推理看似例外，但实为推理模型在思维链中直接解题而非进行元认知评估的混淆效应。研究未发现任何测试领域存在显著的言语化个体元认知证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量关于校准度的研究，但对模型置信度的内在结构（即诱导出的置信度是否具有功能元认知属性）理解不足。本文旨在探究大语言模型的言语化置信度是否真正反映其对自身能力的认知，还是仅基于任务难度等共享因素的简单映射。

Method: 采用四因子分析与成对校准相结合的方法，对20个前沿大语言模型在六个基准上的二元置信度判断进行分解分析。通过检验交叉模型置信度矩阵的秩结构、提取主导因子、去除共同一致项以及控制基率差异，系统考察置信度与性能之间的关系。

Result: 在事实回忆和信息检索基准上，交叉模型置信度矩阵近似秩一，单一因子主导。去除模型一致认同项后置信度-性能关系消失。模型间成对校准效应即使显著也很小，控制共享因子基率差异后归零。数学推理的显著效应实为推理模型在思维链中直接解题的混淆。

Conclusion: 当前前沿大语言模型的言语化置信度判断并不反映真正的功能元认知能力——即独立于执行任务本身而评估自身能力的能力。模型间的置信度差异主要由任务难度这个共享因子上的不同决策阈值导致，而非个体化的自我认知。

Abstract: Confidence-weighted routing, selective abstention, and ensemble weighting all assume that a model's stated confidence is informative about its capability on the question being asked. They presume functional metacognition, the capacity to assess one's own capabilities, without exercising them. Aggregate calibration is well studied, with mixed results, but the underlying structure of elicited confidence is less well understood. We decompose binary confidence judgements from 20 frontier Large Language Models (LLMs) across six benchmarks using tetrachoric factor analysis paired with pairwise calibration, asking whether two models that differ in confidence also differ in performance. On factual recall and information retrieval benchmarks the cross-model confidence matrix is approximately rank-one and a single dominant factor captures most of the latent variance. Models retrieving facts share an item-level difficulty axis and differ mainly in their decision thresholds along it. Across all benchmarks the relationship between confidence and performance collapses once items that all models agree on are removed. Inter-model pairwise calibration is small even where statistically significant, and what remains shrinks to nothing once base-rate differences along the shared factor are controlled for. Mathematical reasoning is the apparent exception, but this turns out to be a confound where reasoning models answer questions about their confidence by trying to solve them in their chain of thought, bypassing the sub-symbolic self-knowledge we seek to measure. We find no evidence for significant verbalised individuated metacognition in any tested domain.

</details>


### [97] [ChaosBench-Logic v2: Evaluating LLM Logical Reasoning over Dynamical Systems at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24305)
*Noel Thomas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ChaosBench-Logic v2基准（40,886题，165个动力系统）和CARE评估协议，发现前沿模型在状态转移推理上接近随机（MCC=0.05），一阶逻辑演绎达MCC=0.52，部分模型在分岔问题上出现系统性负相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准准确率指标无法揭示二值推理中的三种关键失败模式：先验崩塌、释义不一致、参数依赖动力学推理缺陷，需要更系统的评估方法。

Method: 构建含27个FOL谓词和78条公理边的ChaosBench-Logic v2基准，提出CARE评估协议，对14个模型进行多维度病理学分析。

Result: 状态转移推理MCC=0.05（接近随机），FOL演绎MCC=0.52；闭源模型优势集中在跨指标（+0.40）和一致性任务；Qwen 2.5-32B在指标诊断上领先（0.91 vs 0.45）；两个模型在分岔问题上出现负MCC。

Conclusion: 现有模型在动力系统推理中存在系统性缺陷，标准评估指标具有误导性，需要更细粒度的诊断框架。

Abstract: Standard accuracy on binary reasoning benchmarks hides critical failure modes: prior collapse, inconsistency under paraphrase, and inability to reason about parameter-dependent dynamics. We present ChaosBench-Logic v2, a 40,886-question benchmark over 165 dynamical systems with 27 FOL predicates and 78 axiom edges, together with CARE (Calibration- and Adversarial-Robust Evaluation), a protocol that surfaces these pathologies. Evaluating 14 models, we find that regime-transition reasoning remains near random (MCC = 0.05) even for frontier models, whereas FOL deduction with given premises reaches MCC = 0.52. Per-family decomposition shows that the proprietary-model advantage concentrates on cross-indicator (+0.40) and consistency tasks, while open-source Qwen 2.5-32B dominates indicator diagnostics (0.91 vs. 0.45). Two models exhibit negative MCC on bifurcation questions, confirmed as systematic anti-correlation via confusion-matrix analysis.

</details>


### [98] [From One-Pass SGD to Data Reuse: Mini-Batch Scaling Laws in Sketched Linear Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24316)
*Ziyan Chen,Ding-Xuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机草图线性回归中批量大小对缩放定律的影响，推导出一遍批量SGD、带替换多遍批量SGD和不带替换多遍批量SGD的风险分解与源条件缩放定律，揭示批大小对随机项的不同影响机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缩放定律主要关注单样本SGD或全数据重用场景，小批量在缩放定律中的作用尚不明确，亟需系统理论分析。

Method: 基于幂律协方差谱和目标参数源条件，对三种采样协议下的批SGD进行风险分解，推导缩放定律与波动协方差表达式。

Result: 1）三种方法共享不可约项和逼近项，仅随机项不同；2）一遍批SGD中批大小效应被缩短的优化视野部分抵消；3）无替换采样比有替换采样噪声更小，当B=N时波动消失退化为确定性梯度下降。

Conclusion: 将批大小与计算量、数据量、模型维度置于相同的理论基础上，为实践中的批大小选择提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Scaling laws provide compact descriptions of how prediction error varies with compute, model size, and data, but existing theory mainly treats single-sample SGD or full data reuse, leaving the role of mini-batching unclear. We study batch scaling laws for sketched linear regression under a power-law covariance spectrum and a source condition on the target parameter. We analyze one-pass batch SGD, multi-pass batch SGD with replacement, and multi-pass batch SGD without replacement. Our first result is a risk decomposition: all three procedures share the same irreducible and approximation terms, while their stochastic terms depend on the sampling protocol. One-pass batch SGD splits into bias and variance, whereas the two multi-pass methods split into GD bias, GD variance, and a fluctuation term around a common GD reference trajectory. We then prove source-condition scaling laws for one-pass and multi-pass mini-batch methods. For one-pass batch SGD, mini-batching preserves the approximation and optimization-bias exponents, while the variance scales as $O(\min(M,(T_{\mathrm{eff}}γ)^{1/a})/(B T_{\mathrm{eff}}))$. Thus the usual $1/B$ covariance reduction holds at fixed update count $T$, but in the one-pass regime $T=N/B$ it is partly offset by the shorter optimization horizon. For multi-pass batch SGD, with- and without-replacement sampling have identical approximation and GD bias/variance terms; they differ only in the fluctuation covariance prefactor, which is $1/B$ with replacement and $ρ_{N,B}=(N-B)/(B(N-1))$ without replacement. Hence without-replacement sampling is less noisy for $B>1$, and when $B=N$ the fluctuation vanishes, recovering deterministic gradient descent. These results place batch size on the same theoretical footing as compute, data, and model dimension in sketched linear regression.

</details>


### [99] [EMA-Nesterov: Stabilizing Nesterov's Lookahead for Accelerated Deep Learning Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25395)
*Chung-Yiu Yau,Dawei Li,Athanasios Glentis,Valentyn Boreiko,Hoi-To Wai,Mingyi Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EMA-Nesterov方法，用指数移动平均（EMA）替代传统Nesterov加速中的短期一步更新信号作为前瞻方向，通过低通滤波捕捉训练轨迹的低频趋势，在保持理论加速收敛速率的同时，在语言模型预训练等任务中优于Adam、SOAP、Muon等基础优化器，解决了标准Nesterov因随机梯度噪声和非凸损失景观导致的不稳定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Nesterov加速方法在深度学习训练中因随机梯度噪声和非凸损失景观而变得不可靠，其依赖的短期一步更新信号（相邻迭代差异）本质上是噪声的，导致外推方向不稳定。作者旨在利用优化轨迹的低频趋势来获得稳定的加速方向。

Method: 提出EMA-Nesterov，将标准Nesterov的前瞻方向替换为参数更新的指数移动平均（EMA），通过低通滤波器捕捉训练轨迹的演化趋势，同时利用EMA的几何加权结构保持对渐进变化的自适应性。

Result: 在凸问题中保留了与Nesterov加速梯度方法类似的理论加速收敛速率。在语言模型预训练（NanoGPT）中，EMA-Nesterov在Adam、SOAP、Muon等多种基础优化器上均取得更好性能，优于既有的短视窗前瞻方法和长视窗前瞻方法。

Conclusion: EMA-Nesterov通过稳定化的前瞻方向有效避免了短视窗前瞻的不稳定性和长视窗前瞻的非自适应性，是一种广泛适用的深度学习优化加速方法。

Abstract: Lookahead-based acceleration methods, such as Nesterov's momentum, are widely used in optimization, but they often become unreliable in deep learning training mainly due to stochastic gradient noise and non-convex loss landscapes. In particular, standard lookahead relies on short-horizon update signals (e.g., differences between consecutive iterates), which are inherently noisy and can lead to unstable extrapolation directions. This work revisits Nesterov's acceleration from a trajectory perspective and argues that effective acceleration in deep learning should harness the low-frequency trends of optimization trajectories rather than extrapolating noisy one-step updates. Leveraging this insight, we propose EMA-Nesterov, a simple modification that replaces the standard Nesterov's lookahead direction with an exponential moving average (EMA) of parameter updates. This yields a stabilized lookahead direction that captures and harnesses the evolving trend of the training trajectory through a low-pass filter, while remaining adaptive to progressive changes via the geometric weighting structure of EMA. We show that EMA-Nesterov retains a theoretical accelerated convergence rate in convex problems that is analogous to Nesterov's accelerated gradient method. Furthermore, we provide empirical evidence on language model pre-training to verify that EMA-Nesterov is broadly applicable across a range of fine-tuned base optimizers, including Adam, SOAP, Muon, as well as complex optimizers that achieve state-of-the-art performance on optimization benchmarks (NanoGPT). Compared to prior lookahead methods, EMA-Nesterov achieves better performance by avoiding the instability of short-horizon lookahead and the non-adaptivity of long-horizon lookahead.

</details>


### [100] [Omissive Bias in Religious Representation: Benchmarking LLM Answers to Everyday Ethical Decision-making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24319)
*David Wingate,Sheryl Carty,Joshua Coates,Daniel Feldman,Nancy Fulda,Larry Howell,Brett Israelson,Dallin Jacobs,Jonathan Karr,John Paul Kimes,Elisabeth Kincaid,Paul Martens,Gavin Mobley,Suzana Pinheiro,Lindsay Slemboski,Peter Whiting*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出“遗漏性偏见”概念，通过AllFaith基准测试评估27个LLM，发现模型在回答伦理和个人问题时系统性地低估了宗教视角，尤其在实用生活场景中比人类预期更少提及宗教。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM正成为指导个人、道德和存在问题的默认来源，但尚不清楚它们是否系统性地省略了历史上塑造此类推理的宗教框架。作者希望衡量LLM中宗教表述的缺失，将其作为价值对齐和偏见的一个维度。

Method: 构建AllFaith宗教表述基准：150个来自真实聊天记录和宗教社区贡献者的伦理与个人问题，搭配LLM-as-judge评估标准（提及任何宗教、宗教实践或宗教领袖即给满分）。同时进行人类受试者调查，比较LLM行为与人类期望。

Result: 评估27个模型发现：LLM一致性地低估宗教表述，低于人类预期；这种遗漏具有不对称性——模型对抽象存在性问题（意义、死亡、真理）更易提及宗教，而对实用个人场景（悲伤、婚姻、家庭冲突、成瘾）提及更少。

Conclusion: 并非要裁定LLM应持何种价值观，而是指出当前LLM的回答忽视了反映许多人在应对个人和伦理挑战时所依赖的宗教框架的关键机会。

Abstract: As large language models become a default source of guidance on personal, moral, and existential questions, it matters whether they draw on the religious frameworks that have historically shaped such reasoning, or systematically omit them. In this paper, we ask a deliberately narrow question: when posed an everyday ethical question for which religious perspectives may be valuable, do LLMs invoke religion at all? In contrast to benchmarks that look for the presence of political leanings or social bias, we look for the absence of religious representation as a dimension of value alignment and bias in LLMs. We term this ``omissive bias.''
  To measure omissive bias, we contribute the AllFaith Religious Representation Benchmark: 150 ethically and personally salient questions, sourced from in-the-wild chat transcripts and faith-community contributors, paired with an LLM-as-judge rubric that gives full credit for any mention of a religion, a religious practice, or a religious leader. The questions are not themselves about religion--they are open-ended questions about grief, forgiveness, relationships, purpose, and honesty, where religion is one valuable perspective among several. We also run a human-subjects survey to compare LLM behavior against human expectations.
  Evaluating 27 models, we find that LLMs consistently underrepresent religion relative to human expectations. The omission is asymmetric: models invoke religion more readily for abstract existential questions (meaning, death, truth) than for the practical personal situations--grief, marriage, family conflict, addiction--where many people most rely on it. It is not our purpose to adjudicate which values LLMs should hold. We argue, more modestly, that current LLM responses overlook critical opportunities to reflect religious frameworks that many people draw on when navigating personal and ethical challenges.

</details>


### [101] [CurveRL: Principled Distribution-Aware Context Reweighting for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24331)
*Ke Sun,Yizhou Zhao,Jiayi Xin,Qi Long,Weijie Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CurveRL，一种基于分位坐标变换的分布感知提示重加权方法，用于提升大语言模型在带验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）中的推理能力，通过将提示重加权形式化为通过率函数空间上效用泛函的泛函导数，建立了统一的最优性框架，实验表明CurveRL在多个基准上持续优于GRPO等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管上下文或提示级别的重加权已成为RLVR中提升大语言模型推理能力的关键算法手段，但决定最优加权的原则尚不明确，现有方法缺乏统一的理论理解。

Method: 将提示重加权形式化为通过率函数空间上效用泛函的泛函导数，建立统一最优性框架；在此基础上提出CurveRL，采用分位坐标变换实现分布感知的提示重加权，权重取决于通过率的排序和密度而非绝对值。

Result: 在多个基准上，CurveRL持续优于GRPO及其他RLVR基线方法。

Conclusion: 上下文分布控制是分析和设计提示重加权RLVR算法的一个原则性轴心，代码已开源。

Abstract: Context or prompt-level reweighting has emerged as a central algorithmic lever in Reinforcement Learning with Verified Rewards (RLVR) for improving the reasoning capability of large language models, yet the principle determining what constitutes an optimal weighting remains poorly understood. We address this gap by formulating prompt reweighting as a functional derivative of a utility functional defined in the pass-rate function space, yielding a unified optimality framework that accommodates existing schemes, including REINFORCE and GRPO. Building on this optimality framework, we propose a distribution-aware prompt reweighting approach, called CurveRL, based on a quantile coordinate transform, in which the weight assigned to each prompt depends not on the absolute value of pass rates but on its rank and density to reflect the distributional structure of the pass rates in the learning dynamics. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that our proposed CurveRL consistently outperforms GRPO and other RLVR baselines. Our study identifies context-distribution control as a principled axis for analyzing and designing prompt-reweighted RLVR algorithms. The code is released in https://github.com/zhyzmath/CurveRL.

</details>


### [102] [Evolving Robustness--Exploration Trade-off in Online Reinforcement Learning via Quantile Bayesian Risk MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24345)
*Meichen Song,Yuhao Wang,Enlu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线强化学习中的时变鲁棒性与探索性权衡问题，提出基于分位数贝叶斯风险感知MDP的框架，通过自适应分位数调度实现早期鲁棒、后期探索的策略，并证明了次线性贝叶斯遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线强化学习中，数据稀缺导致认知不确定性，使得鲁棒性在学习初期至关重要，同时需要充分探索以学习真实环境的最优策略，因此需要研究随时间变化的鲁棒性-探索性权衡。

Method: 提出分位数贝叶斯风险感知MDP（BR-MDP）框架，利用分位数水平控制后验不确定性进入Bellman回溯的程度。通过渐近正态性结果刻画分位数BR-MDP值与真实环境值的差异，表明上/下尾分位数分别诱导对认知不确定性的乐观/悲观态度，且乐观/悲观程度随数据积累而降低。基于此提出自适应分位数调度的在线贝叶斯风险感知算法。

Result: 建立了关于真实最优值和最优BR-MDP鲁棒值的次线性贝叶斯遗憾界。数值实验表明算法在探索需求高和探索成本高的环境中均表现优异。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应分位数调度方法能有效平衡早期鲁棒性和后期探索性需求，理论分析与实验验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: In online reinforcement learning, data scarcity creates epistemic uncertainty that makes robustness important early in learning, whereas sufficient exploration is needed to learn the true-environment optimal policy. We study this time-varying robustness--exploration trade-off through a quantile Bayesian risk-aware Markov decision process (BR-MDP), in which the quantile level controls how posterior uncertainty enters the Bellman backup. We characterize this control through an asymptotic normality result for the difference between the quantile BR-MDP value and the value in the true environment. The result implies that upper/lower-tail quantiles induce optimism/pessimism towards epistemic uncertainty, and the magnitude of the optimism/pessimism decreases as data accumulate. Building on this characterization, we propose an online Bayesian risk-aware algorithm with an adaptive quantile schedule that emphasizes robustness early and gradually encourages exploration of less-visited state--action pairs. We establish sublinear Bayesian regret bounds with respect to both the true optimal value and the optimal BR-MDP robust value. Numerical experiments demonstrate strong performance in both exploration-demanding and exploration-costly environments.

</details>


### [103] [Refined Analysis of Entropy-Regularized Actor-Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24357)
*Safwan Labbi,Paul Mangold,Daniil Tiapkin,Eric Moulines*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了熵正则化有限折扣环境中actor-critic方法中critic的作用，证明当critic精确时，将其作为baseline是强方差缩减方法，使随机梯度actor-critic达到与确定性策略梯度相当的样本复杂度（$\tilde{O}(\log(1/\epsilon))$）。当critic误差足够小时，方差缩减和快速收敛得以保持，建议先学习critic并在每次actor更新后保持其更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解critic在actor-critic方法中的理论作用，特别是其作为baseline对方差缩减和样本复杂度的影响，以及实践中critic学习误差对算法性能的影响。

Method: 理论分析：在熵正则化有限折扣MDP环境下，分析精确critic作为baseline时的方差缩减性质；推导随机梯度actor-critic的样本复杂度界；分析critic估计误差对actor更新方差和收敛性的影响。

Result: 1) 精确critic作为baseline是强方差缩减方法；2) 在此条件下，随机梯度actor-critic达到$\tilde{O}(\log(1/\epsilon))$样本复杂度；3) 当critic误差足够小时，方差缩减和快速收敛性质得以保持。

Conclusion: critic的精确估计在actor-critic方法中至关重要，建议优先学习critic并在每次actor更新后保持其更新，以确保方差缩减和快速收敛。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the role of the critic in actor--critic for entropy-regularized, finite, discounted environments. We establish that, when the critic is exact, using the latter as a baseline is a variance-reduction method in a strong sense. In this case, actor--critic with stochastic gradients matches the sample complexity of deterministic policy gradient, reaching an $ε$-optimal regularized value with $\tilde{O}(\log(1/ε))$ samples. In practice, the critic is learned alongside the actor: the variance of the actor update is then influenced by the critic's variance and bias. Specifically, when the critic has a sufficiently small error, the variance reduction and rapid convergence are preserved. This suggests to learn the critic first, keeping it up to date after each actor update, underscoring the crucial role of accurate critic estimation in actor--critic methods.

</details>


### [104] [GEESE: Genotype-aware End-to-End Spatio-temporal Embedding for Behavioral Phenotyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24370)
*Yiran Ding,Yuen Gao,Chunqi Qian,Zijun Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEESE是一个端到端深度学习框架，利用预训练时间序列基础模型从3D姿态动力学中学习行为表征，在三种自闭症相关基因模型上超越传统手工特征方法，同时开发了HONK自然语言交互工具供非编程研究人员使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前遗传动物模型的行为表型分析依赖耗时的手工特征工程，限制了可重复性和可扩展性，亟需一种端到端的自动化行为表征学习方法。

Method: 提出GEESE框架，使用预训练时间序列基础模型从3D姿态动力学中编码行为流形，支持行为分类和基因型预测，并开发HONK工具实现自然语言交互。

Result: 在CNTNAP2、CHD8、FMR1三种自闭症基因模型上，深度学习方法在行为分类和基因型预测两项任务中均超越手工特征基线，且框架可跨遗传背景泛化；全队列模型仅凭运动模式即可区分遗传背景和基因型。

Conclusion: 学习到的表征能够捕获基因型特异性行为特征，框架具有良好的泛化能力，HONK工具降低了非编程研究人员的使用门槛。

Abstract: Behavioral phenotyping of genetic animal models currently requires labor-intensive manual feature engineering that limits reproducibility and scalability. We present GEESE, an end-to-end deep learning framework that learns behavioral representations directly from 3D pose dynamics without hand-crafted features. Using a pretrained time series foundation model, we encode movement sequences into a behavioral manifold that supports both behavior classification and genotype prediction. Evaluated across three autism-associated genetic models (CNTNAP2, CHD8, FMR1), our deep learning approach surpasses hand-crafted feature baselines in both tasks, revealing that learned representations capture genotype-specific behavioral signatures. The framework generalizes across genetic backgrounds, and an all-cohort model identifies both genetic background and genotype from movement patterns alone. We further provide HONK, an interactive intelligent tool enabling researchers without programming expertise to perform behavioral phenotyping from pose data through natural language interaction.

</details>


### [105] [Assessing the Operational Viability of Foundation Models for Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24381)
*Kavin Soni,Debanshu Das,Vamshi Guduguntla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了时序基础模型与监督学习方法在四种操作场景下的性能表现，并提出了一种基于特征选择的最优模型分配框架（Complexity Router），可在提升精度的同时显著降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序预测在金融、交通、能源等领域驱动运营决策，监督学习方法虽然表现优异但需要领域特定的训练、特征工程和持续维护；而大规模基础模型作为零样本替代方案虽避免了任务特定训练，但其适用边界尚不明确。本文旨在系统评估两类方法在不同操作场景下的优劣，为实际部署提供指导。

Method: 本文评估了基础模型与监督学习方法在四种操作场景（周期性人本系统、物理约束过程、随机金融市场、异质需求预测）下的性能，并从推理延迟、数据漂移适应性、部署约束等维度量化权衡。进一步提出Complexity Router，利用序列经验特征将每条时间序列分配给最优模型类别，实现选择性路由。

Result: 基础模型在具有可迁移周期结构的领域表现良好，适合冷启动或长尾场景；监督专家在严格物理约束的系统中保持更高精度；在金融领域，新型基础模型正快速缩小与监督专家的差距。选择性路由相较于部署单一通用基础模型，实现了更高精度和显著更低的推理成本。

Conclusion: 时序基础模型和监督学习方法各有最佳适用场景，通过Complexity Router进行针对性模型选择可以在泛化性与效率之间取得更好平衡，为实际部署提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Time series forecasting drives operational decisions in areas like finance, transportation, and energy. While supervised learning approaches achieve strong performance, they require domain-specific training, feature engineering, and ongoing maintenance. Large-scale foundation models have recently emerged as a zero-shot alternative, avoiding task-specific training much like LLMs. In this work, we evaluate foundation models against standard supervised approaches. Rather than focusing solely on aggregate accuracy, we analyze performance across four operational regimes: periodic human-centric systems, physically constrained processes, stochastic financial markets, and heterogeneous demand forecasting.
  Our results characterize optimal deployment areas. Foundation models perform well in domains with transferable periodic structures and are efficient for cold-start or long-tail scenarios. Conversely, supervised specialists maintain higher precision in systems governed by strict physical constraints. In financial domains, newer foundation models are rapidly closing the performance gap with supervised specialists. We further quantify trade-offs in inference latency, data drift adaptability, and deployment constraints. Finally, we propose a Complexity Router that assigns each series to the optimal model class using empirical features. We demonstrate that this selective routing achieves higher accuracy and significantly lower inference costs compared to deploying a universal foundation model, providing a practical framework for balancing generalization and efficiency.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [106] [Variational Contraction Conditions for Iterative Algorithms in Multi-Population Discrete-Time Regularized Mean-Field Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.23906)
*Uğur Aydın,Tamer Başar*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有多个种群的离散时间正则化平均场博弈中迭代算法的收缩条件，给出了基于Lipschitz参数的显式收缩条件，并提供了有限时域均衡收敛到无限时域均衡的速率，在单种群情况下比现有结果限制更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多个种群相互作用导致的高维度使得迭代算法的收缩率无法用根式表达，现有方法难以处理，因此需要更通用的收缩条件分析框架。

Method: 通过研究迭代算法的动力学，假设每个种群MFG的系统分量满足Lipschitz连续性，在任意赋范空间中给出基于Lipschitz参数的显式（最终）收缩条件；利用变分刻画约束下的动力学性质，推导有限时域均衡到无限时域均衡的收敛速率。

Result: 提出了任意赋范空间下多种群MFG迭代算法的显式收缩条件；给出了有限时域MFE收敛到无限时域平稳（及非平稳）MFE的收敛速率；单种群情况下所需变分刻画约束弱于现有文献。

Conclusion: 本文建立的多种群MFG迭代算法收缩条件为高维交互系统的收敛性分析提供了理论保证，且在单种群情形改进了已有结果。

Abstract: In this work, we study the contraction conditions of iterative algorithms for stationary and finite-horizon discrete-time regularized mean-field games (MFGs) with multiple populations, where each population only interacts with the state distributions of the other populations. Due to the high dimensionality caused by the interaction of different populations, contraction rates for these algorithms cannot, in general, be expressed in terms of radicals. By studying the dynamics of these iterative algorithms and assuming that the system components of each population's MFG are Lipschitz continuous, we present explicit (eventual) contraction conditions for each algorithm in any normed space, relying only on these Lipschitz parameters. As a consequence of these contraction conditions, we provide convergence rates of finite-horizon mean-field equilibria to infinite-horizon stationary (and non-stationary) mean-field equilibria (MFEs), under restrictions on a variational characterization of the dynamics of these iterative algorithms. In the single-population case, the restrictions we impose on this variational characterization to obtain these convergence results are less restrictive than previous results in the literature.

</details>


### [107] [Threshold-Safe Shock Absorption in a Compartmental Voter-Flow Model:\ A Conservative Impulse-Control Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24186)
*Alexander Omelchenko*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对简化的选民流（compartmental voter-flow）模型，提出了确定性阈值安全（deterministic threshold-safety）问题。该模型包含一个带有指数恢复的外生负荷蓄积池（alienation reservoir）。研究发现，在主流的基线附近，系统存在线性稳定性阈值Δ_c：低于该阈值时动员部分收缩，高于该阈值则可能产生瞬态放大。论文引入了该阈值机制的脉冲控制层，将阈值与局部稳定性边界关联，将暴露函数与正对数放大相联系，并证明了标量蓄积池模型是非线性选民流动动力学的一个保守包络。基于此框架，推导出了多种显式安全基准：单次释放暴露及其零缓冲、固定单次开销下的完全松弛分割问题、有限恢复恒定峰值剖面，以及固定时间视界下的容量前沿Δ_c(1+ρT)。简化后的标量递推方程本质上是经典的泄漏蓄积池（leaky-reservoir）骨架，该结构也出现在多剂量药代动力学、分次放疗、水库调度以及设置成本调度等领域。该模型的核心贡献在于使区室化冲击吸收模型中的阈值机制变得解析透明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是建立一种确定性阈值安全分析框架，用于理解选民流动（voter-flow）类社交动力学系统中，当系统受到外生负荷（如信息冲击）时，系统是否以及在什么条件下会进入不稳定或激增（放大）状态。作者希望将复杂的非线性动态系统简化为一个易于解析处理的标量蓄积池模型，从而得出显式的安全操作边界。

Method: 论文采用的方法包括：建立简化的区室化选民流动模型（包含 alienation reservoir 和指数恢复）；通过线性稳定性分析确定阈值Δ_c；引入脉冲控制层（impulse-control layer）来管理该阈值机制；将暴露函数（exposure functional）与正对数放大相关联；证明标量蓄积池模型是非线性动力学的保守包络（conservative envelope）；最后在该标量框架下导出多种安全基准解（如单次释放、完全松弛、恒定峰值、固定视界容量前沿等）。

Result: 主要结果是得到了多个显式的安全基准表达式：(1) 单次释放暴露及其零缓冲条件；(2) 固定单次开销下的完全松弛分割问题；(3) 有限恢复恒定峰值剖面；(4) 固定时间视界的容量前沿，具体形式为Δ_c(1+ρT)。此外，论文证明了简化后的标量递推方程（leaky-reservoir 骨架）在数学形式上与药代动力学、放疗、水库调度等领域的经典模型同构。

Conclusion: 论文的结论是：通过将复杂的区室化选民流动模型约化为一个标量泄漏蓄积池模型，能够对系统的阈值安全区域进行完整的解析刻画。这一框架不仅为社交动力学中的冲击吸收问题提供了明确的定量安全界限，还揭示了该问题与多个成熟学科（药代动力学、放疗、水资源调度等）中的经典模型在结构上的统一性，从而增强了跨学科的理论迁移潜力。

Abstract: We formulate a deterministic threshold-safety problem for a reduced compartmental voter-flow model. An exogenous load enters an alienation reservoir; between releases the reservoir recovers exponentially. Near the mainstream baseline the compartmental dynamics have a linear-stability threshold \(Δ_c\): below this level the mobilised component contracts, while above it transient amplification is possible. The paper introduces an impulse-control layer for this threshold mechanism. The threshold is obtained from the local stability boundary of the reduced dynamical system, the exposure functional is tied to positive logarithmic amplification, and the scalar reservoir model is proved to be a conservative envelope of the nonlinear voter-flow dynamics. This bridge yields explicit safety benchmarks: the single-release exposure and its zero buffer, the complete-relaxation splitting problem with fixed per-release overhead, the finite-recovery constant-peak profile, and the fixed-horizon capacity frontier \(Δ_c(1+ρT)\). The scalar recurrence used after the reduction is the familiar leaky-reservoir skeleton also found in multiple-dose pharmacokinetics, fractionated radiotherapy, reservoir operation, and setup-cost scheduling. Its role here is to make the threshold regimes of the compartmental shock-absorption model analytically transparent.

</details>


### [108] [Single-Chord Augmentation of Weighted Cycles for Algebraic Connectivity and Network Coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24479)
*Jiarong Deng,Liu Chang,Quanshun Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究在加权环状通信图中添加单条加权弦以同时提升共识收敛速率和稳态不一致水平的问题，揭示了弦的电阻分裂机制，推导了精确的性能更新公式，证明了近对径电阻平衡弦在代数连通度提升上的近最优性，并设计了RBAPS和AW-RBAPS两种筛选规则以实现高效帕累托优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无人机编队、循环巡逻、周界监控等多智能体任务中，环状通信图广泛存在。当测量和执行噪声持续存在时，需要同时提升共识的收敛速率（代数连通度）和降低稳态不一致水平（基尔霍夫指数），而添加弦是一种有效的图增强手段，但弦对这两个目标的影响机制尚不清晰。

Method: 1) 推导了弦诱导的有效电阻和基尔霍夫指数精确更新公式，建立闭环的相干性目标函数；2) 证明有界电导和小电阻差异条件下，近对径电阻平衡弦在代数连通度提升上近最优，并通过i.i.d.有界电导模型验证高概率结论；3) 将设计建模为有限帕累托问题，提出RBAPS和AW-RBAPS两种电阻平衡筛选规则，将候选集规模降至线性或近线性。

Result: AW-RBAPS在正式的中等异质性假设之外仍保持有效，平均超体积比达0.9987，仅评估约10.1%的可行弦即可逼近穷举帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: 弦的效应由其对环的电阻分裂决定，而非简单的秩一边更新；电阻平衡的弦能同时优化两个目标，且所提筛选规则可在保持近最优性能的同时大幅降低计算复杂度。

Abstract: Ring-like communication graphs appear in UAV formations, cyclic patrols, perimeter monitoring, and other multi-agent tasks in which agents exchange information mainly with neighboring vehicles along a closed route. When measurement and actuation noise are persistent, a useful augmentation should improve both the convergence rate of consensus and the steady-state disagreement level. This paper studies the addition of a single weighted chord to a connected weighted cycle. The central observation is that a chord is not just a generic rank-one edge update: it splits the cycle into two complementary resistance arcs, and this resistance split governs both the algebraic-connectivity gain and the Kirchhoff-index reduction. We first derive exact chord-induced effective-resistance and Kirchhoff-index update formulas, giving a closed-form coherence objective. We then prove that, under bounded conductances and small resistance discrepancy, near-antipodal resistance-balanced chords are near-optimal for algebraic-connectivity improvement; an i.i.d. bounded-conductance model yields the same conclusion with high probability. Finally, because the best convergence-rate chord and the best coherence chord need not coincide, we formulate the design as a finite Pareto problem and introduce RBAPS and AW-RBAPS, two resistance-balanced screening rules that retain only linear or near-linear candidate sets. Numerical experiments show that AW-RBAPS remains effective beyond the formal moderate-heterogeneity regime and approximates the exhaustive Pareto front with mean hypervolume ratio $0.9987$ while evaluating about $10.1\%$ of admissible chords.

</details>


### [109] [Optimal drift optimizer for non-convex optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24485)
*Qin Li,Sixu Li,Eitan Tadmor,Emmanuel Trélat*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究有限时域随机控制准则下的非凸优化问题，通过平衡布朗探索与二次控制成本，导出最优过程的条件终端分布为近端惩罚能量的吉布斯测度，并得到三种精确漂移表示。分析了终端时间趋近和低温两种渐近区域，证明低温下条件终端分布集中于全局极小值点，漂移收敛至仿射吸引场，并给出无梯度离散化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将随机控制理论应用于非凸优化问题，通过布朗探索与二次控制成本的平衡，揭示最优漂移的结构，为全局优化提供理论支撑。

Method: 从有限时域随机控制准则出发，利用条件终端分布的吉布斯测度性质，推导出势、平均梯度和重心三种精确漂移表示；在终端时间趋近和低温渐近区域分析漂移行为，并给出Laplace渐近结果。

Result: 1) 条件终端分布为近端惩罚能量的吉布斯测度；2) 获得势、平均梯度、重心三种精确漂移表示；3) 终端时间趋近时漂移恢复梯度下降场；4) 低温下条件终端分布集中于全局极小值点，漂移收敛至仿射吸引场；5) 非退化情形下得到漂移、值函数和协方差的Laplace渐近；6) 基于重心公式提出简单无梯度离散化方法。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了随机控制与全局优化之间的深层联系，通过吉布斯测度和三种漂移表示为非凸优化提供了新的理论框架，低温渐近分析和无梯度离散化具有良好的实际应用潜力。

Abstract: We study a finite-horizon stochastic control criterion for non-convex optimization in which Brownian exploration is balanced against a quadratic control cost. Rather than emphasizing the classical Hopf--Cole representation, we isolate the exact drift selected by the criterion and reorganize it in a form adapted to optimization. The key object is the conditional terminal law of the optimal process. We show that this law is a Gibbs measure for a proximally penalized energy, yielding three exact representations of the drift: potential, averaged-gradient, and barycentric. We then analyze two asymptotic regimes relevant for optimization. As terminal time is approached, the drift recovers a scaled gradient-descent field. In the low-temperature regime, assuming a unique global minimizer, the conditional terminal law concentrates on it even in the presence of nonglobal local minima, and the drift converges to an affine attraction field toward it. In the nondegenerate case we also derive Laplace asymptotics for the drift, the value function, and the covariance of the conditional terminal law. Finally, we record a simple gradient-free discretization suggested by the barycentric formula.

</details>


### [110] [Orthant-Monotonic Norms and Additive D-Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24567)
*Ron Ofir,Michael Margaliot*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了由向量范数诱导的矩阵测度的可容许性（即在任意非负对角阻尼下保持负性），证明了矩阵测度可容许当且仅当底层范数是象限单调的，等价于对任意非负对角矩阵D满足μ(D)=max_i{d_ii}，并指出该类在非负对角扰动下一致保持收缩性的意义上是极大的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性动力系统收缩理论中广泛使用矩阵测度，一个自然且重要的鲁棒性问题是：矩阵测度的负性是否能在任意非负对角阻尼下保持不变。具有此性质的矩阵测度被称为可容许的，本文旨在给出其充要刻画。

Method: 通过理论分析建立矩阵测度可容许性与范数几何性质之间的等价关系，利用象限单调性（orthant-monotonicity）给出充要条件，并进一步证明该结论在一致保持收缩性的意义下是最优的。

Result: 证明了诱导矩阵测度可容许当且仅当底层范数是象限单调的，等价于μ(D)=max_i{d_ii}对任意非负对角矩阵D成立。该刻画在非负对角扰动一致保持收缩性的意义下是极大的。

Conclusion: 给出了可容许性的新几何刻画，阐明了象限单调性在加性D-稳定性和扩散诱导不稳定性中的作用。

Abstract: Matrix measures induced by vector norms are widely used in contraction theory of nonlinear dynamical systems. A natural and important robustness question is whether negativity of a matrix measure is preserved under arbitrary nonnegative diagonal damping. Matrix measures with this property have been called admissible. We show that an induced matrix measure is admissible if and only if the underlying norm is orthant-monotonic. Equivalently, these are precisely the induced matrix measures satisfying $μ(D) = \max_i\{d_{ii} \}$ for every nonnegative diagonal matrix $D$. We further show that this class is maximal for uniform preservation of contraction under nonnegative diagonal perturbations. The result gives a new geometric characterization of admissibility and clarifies the role of orthant-monotonicity in additive $D$-stability and diffusion-induced instability.

</details>


### [111] [An Algorithm for Approximating the Metric Projection onto a Superelliptic Disk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24599)
*Valerian-Alin Fodor,Virgilius-Aurelian Minuta*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种近似计算超椭圆盘（p>1阶Lamé曲线的凸包）上度量投影的新算法，并证明了其收敛性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超椭圆盘（Lamé曲线的凸包）上的度量投影在几何处理、优化和计算机图形学中有重要应用，但现有算法在精度或效率上存在不足，需要一种收敛性有保障的高效近似算法。

Method: 针对阶数p>1的超椭圆盘（由超椭圆方程|x/a|^p + |y/b|^p ≤ 1定义的凸集），提出了一种新的迭代或解析近似算法来计算点到该凸集上的度量投影（欧几里得投影），并给出了收敛性证明。

Result: 算法收敛性得到理论证明，能够在超椭圆盘上有效近似计算度量投影。

Conclusion: 该算法为超椭圆盘上的度量投影问题提供了一种收敛性有保障的新求解途径，具有理论和实践价值。

Abstract: We propose a new algorithm for approximating the metric projection onto a superelliptic disk of order $p>1$, i.e., the convex hull of a superellipse (Lamé curve), and prove its convergence.

</details>


### [112] [Quadratically Regularized Optimal Transport: Localization Bounds and Affine Case Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24644)
*Long Nguyen-Chi,Nam Nguyen,Binh Nguyen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了二次正则化最优传输(QOT)中优化器支撑集围绕Monge耦合集中率的下界，证明在定向Hausdorff距离下集中速率不超过ε^(1/(d+2))，匹配了最优指数猜想；同时证明了QOT值差距控制均方偏差的尺度为ε^(2/(d+2))，并在仿射Brenier情形下导出了尖锐的管状界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管二次正则化在计算最优传输中具有产生稀疏耦合的理论优势，且在一维和上界方面已有进展，但其优化器支撑集围绕Monge耦合的集中率这一基本问题尚未解决。

Method: 通过建立定向Hausdorff距离下支撑集集中率的一般下界，分析QOT值差距与均方偏差的关系，并在仿射Brenier情形下利用自传输稀疏性结果推导点态管状界。

Result: 证明了支撑集围绕Monge图集中的速率下界为ε^(1/(d+2))，QOT值差距以ε^(2/(d+2))尺度控制均方偏差，在仿射Brenier情形下得到ε^(1/(d+2))阶的尖锐点态管状界。

Conclusion: 所建立的下界匹配了Wiesel等人猜想的最优指数，解决了二次正则化最优传输中集中率的关键理论问题，并通过高维实验验证了理论结果。

Abstract: Quadratic regularization has emerged as a potential alternative to the popular entropic regularization in computational optimal transport, offering the theoretical advantage of producing sparse couplings through its hinge density structure. Despite recent progress in one-dimensional settings and general upper bounds, fundamental questions about the localization rate of QOT optimizers around the Monge coupling have remained open. In this work, we establish a general lower bound showing that the support of the QOT optimizer cannot concentrate around the Monge graph faster than order $\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{d+2}}$ in the directed Hausdorff distance, matching the conjectured optimal exponent under standard regularity assumptions in \citet{wiesel2025sparsity}. We also show that the QOT value gap controls the mean-squared deviation $\mathbb E_{π_\varepsilon}\|y-T(x)\|^2$ by the scale of $\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{d+2}}$. As a corollary, in the affine Brenier regime, which includes Gaussian-to-Gaussian transport, we derive a sharp pointwise tube bound of order $\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{d+2}}$ by reducing the problem to self-transport and applying recent self-transport sparsity results. Finally, we validate our theoretical bound with a synthetic experiment in high-dimensional settings.

</details>


### [113] [Robust Ergodic Control of Jump-Diffusion Systems under Drift and Intensity Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24646)
*Abel Azze,Bernardo D'Auria,Giorgio Ferrari*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了同时受连续波动和罕见冲击影响的随机系统的鲁棒遍历奇异控制问题，其中模型在漂移和跳跃强度上存在不确定性。通过熵惩罚扭曲处理模型模糊性，证明了HJB方程简化为可处理的非线性积分微分自由边界问题，最坏情况模型呈现bang-bang形式，最优策略由反射屏障表征。在指数分布跳跃下，问题进一步简化为常微分方程组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决库存管理、现金管理和容量规划等应用中同时存在连续波动和罕见冲击的随机系统控制问题，并考虑模型参数（漂移和跳跃强度）的不确定性（模型模糊性）。

Method: 将问题建模为鲁棒遍历奇异控制问题，通过熵惩罚扭曲处理模型模糊性，形成极大极小问题。利用HJB方程简化非线性积分微分自由边界问题，分析最坏情况模型的bang-bang形式和反射屏障最优策略，并在指数跳跃分布下进一步简化为ODE系统。

Result: HJB方程简化为可处理的非线性积分微分自由边界问题；最坏情况模型具有bang-bang形式；最优策略由反射屏障表征；指数分布跳跃下问题简化为常微分方程组，支持高效数值计算。

Conclusion: 本文为存在模型模糊性的跳跃扩散系统提供了鲁棒遍历奇异控制的理论框架，给出了可计算的最优策略结构，适用于库存管理、现金管理和容量规划等实际场景。

Abstract: We study a regulation problem for stochastic systems subject to both continuous fluctuations and rare but significant shocks, modeled as a jump-diffusion with uncertainty in both the drift and the jump intensity. Such settings arise in applications including inventory control, cash management, and capacity planning.
  We formulate the problem as a robust ergodic singular control problem in which a decision maker applies upward and downward interventions while accounting for model ambiguity through entropy-penalized distortions. The resulting max-min problem involves a long-run average performance criterion.
  We show that the associated Hamilton--Jacobi--Bellman equation reduces to a nonlinear integro-differential free-boundary problem with a tractable structure. The worst-case model exhibits a bang-bang form, and the optimal policy is characterized by reflecting barriers. Under exponentially distributed jumps, the problem further reduces to a system of ordinary differential equations, enabling efficient numerical computation.

</details>


### [114] [Stochastic maximum principle for optimal control of infinitely delayed systems of functional type in infinite dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24650)
*Guanwei Cheng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究具有无限时滞的随机发展方程的最优控制问题，通过引入非预期路径导数及其无限窗口对偶算子，推导出无限预期倒向随机发展方程作为伴随方程，建立了必要和充分的最大值原理，并应用于无限时滞线性二次型控制问题，得到了显式最优控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无限时滞型随机发展方程的最优控制问题，这类方程在金融数学、人口动力学等领域有重要应用，但现有理论尚未能很好地处理无限时滞情形下的最优控制。

Method: 引入非预期路径导数（non-anticipative path derivative）及其无限窗口对偶算子，推导无限预期倒向随机发展方程作为伴随方程，建立必要和充分的随机最大值原理。

Result: 成功建立了无限时滞随机发展方程的必要和充分最大值原理，并将其应用于无限时滞LQ控制问题，得到了显式最优控制表达式。

Conclusion: 所提出的非预期路径导数框架有效解决了无限时滞随机最优控制问题，为更广泛的应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This paper studies the optimal control problems of stochastic evolution equations with infinite delay of general functional type. By introducing a non-anticipative path derivative and its infinite-window dual operator, we derive the infinitely anticipated backward stochastic evolution equation (IABSEE) as the adjoint equation, and establish both necessary and sufficient maximum principles. As an application, we solve an infinite-delay linear-quadratic (LQ) control problem and obtain the explicit optimal control.

</details>


### [115] [On the geometry of sharp minima](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24766)
*Alberto Domínguez Corella*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文刻画了尖锐极小化子的几何特性，包括倾斜不变性和弱上梯度条件，并将其与非光滑流形中的尖点联系起来，通过切平面滚动与函数倾斜的关联进行解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了深入理解尖锐极小化子的几何本质，而不仅仅停留在代数或分析层面的刻画。

Method: 通过建立倾斜不变性和弱上梯度条件等几何性质来表征尖锐极小化子，并将其与非光滑流形局部表示为图时的尖点现象关联，同时连接切平面滚动与函数倾斜之间的关系。

Result: 给出了尖锐极小化子的几何刻画，揭示了其与倾斜不变性、弱上梯度条件以及非光滑流形尖点之间的内在联系。

Conclusion: 尖锐极小化子具有丰富的几何结构，可以通过倾斜不变性和弱上梯度条件等几何性质来刻画，并且与非光滑流形的尖点现象密切相关。

Abstract: We give characterizations of sharp minimizers that emphasize their geometric properties. These include tilt invariance and weak upper gradient conditions. We relate sharp minimality to cusps in nonsmooth manifolds when interpreted locally as graphs and connect the rolling of tangent planes to the tilting of functions.

</details>


### [116] [Subgradient Methods on Manifolds with Lower Bounded Curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24780)
*G. C. Bento,J. X. Cruz Neto,J. O. Lopes,I. D. L. Melo*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在具有下界曲率的Hadamard流形上，次梯度方法在非光滑凸优化中的收敛性，建立了序列收敛到最优解的充分条件（有界性、解集有界或步长平方可和），并证明了当解集内部非空时算法有限终止，将Shepilov的欧几里得分析推广到了黎曼流形框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受经典欧几里得结果和一阶黎曼优化最新进展的启发，将次梯度方法这一非光滑凸优化的基础算法从欧几里得空间推广到具有下界曲率的Hadamard流形上，填补曲率下界流形上凸最小化问题的理论空白。

Method: 在Hadamard流形（具有下界曲率）上分析次梯度方法的收敛性，采用非可和递减步长条件，分别在有界序列、有界解集、平方可和步长三种情形下证明收敛，并利用解集内部非空条件证明有限终止。

Result: 建立了三种条件下序列收敛到最优解的严格证明；证明了当解集内部非空时算法有限终止；提供了Shepilov欧几里得分析的黎曼对应版本。

Conclusion: 成功将次梯度方法的收敛性理论从欧几里得空间推广到曲率下界有界的Hadamard流形，丰富了流形上凸优化的理论体系。

Abstract: The subgradient method is a classical and foundational approach in non-smooth convex optimization; its simplicity, robustness, and role as a conceptual and algorithmic starting point have made it the backbone of many significant optimization algorithms. Motivated by classical Euclidean results and recent advances in first-order Riemannian optimization, we study the convergence of the subgradient method on Hadamard manifolds with lower bounded curvature. Assuming a nonempty solution set and employing a corresponding non-summable diminishing step-size condition, we establish convergence of the generated sequence $\{x^k\}$ to a minimizer whenever at least one of the following holds: (a) the sequence $\{x^k\}$ is bounded; (b) the solution set $S$ is bounded; or (c) the step-sizes are square-summable ($\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}λ_k^2<\infty$). Additionally, we prove that if $\operatorname{int}(S)\neq\emptyset$, the method achieves finite termination. Our main contribution provides a Riemannian counterpart to Shepilov's Euclidean analysis [Cybernetics, 12 (1976), pp. 544-548], thus complementing existing literature on convex minimization over manifolds with lower bounded curvature.

</details>


### [117] [Lifted Schrödinger Bridges for Gaussian Mixture Endpoints: Projection Gaps and Path-Space Obstructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24795)
*Siddhartha Ganguly,George Rapakoulias,Panagiotis Tsiotras*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在布朗运动先验动力学下，高斯混合端点分布之间的随机密度控制问题。通过引入路径空间提升构造（每条轨迹附加源-目标分量标签），将问题分解为高斯分量间的显式薛定谔桥问题与有限维熵耦合问题的组合，并分析了投影后标签信息损失导致的最优性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯混合端点分布之间的直接薛定谔桥通常无法获得闭式解，需要寻找一种可计算的替代方案来描述两个高斯混合分布之间的最优随机传输路径。

Method: 引入路径空间提升构造，为每条轨迹添加源-目标分量标签，将混合分布问题分解为：1）高斯分量间的显式薛定谔桥（闭式解）；2）有限维熵耦合问题（Sinkhorn缩放形式）。通过投影（丢弃标签）得到原始约束下的解，并分析投影前后相对熵的差异（条件标签信息差距）。

Result: 1）推导了分量间薛定谔桥的显式边际、漂移和代价公式；2）投影后的律满足原始高斯混合端点约束，但相对熵与提升后的相对熵之间存在非负的标签信息差距；3）推导了投影边际流的后验平均马尔可夫漂移，证明了动能上界；4）识别了投影差距消失的路径势条件。

Conclusion: 提升后的优化器在投影后通常不能等同于直接的无标签薛定谔桥；标签信息差距揭示了路径空间中的结构性障碍。通过数值实验验证了密度与形状控制效果。

Abstract: We study stochastic density control between Gaussian-mixture endpoint distributions under Brownian prior dynamics. Since the direct Schrödinger bridge between Gaussian mixtures is generally not available in closed form, we introduce a lifted path-space construction in which each trajectory is augmented with a source--target component label. Consequently, the problem decomposes into Gaussian component-to-component Schrödinger bridges with explicit marginal, drift, and cost formulas, while the mixture-level assignment reduces to a finite-dimensional entropic coupling problem with a Sinkhorn scaling form. We then analyze the projection obtained by discarding or forgetting the label. By construction, the projected law satisfies the original Gaussian-mixture endpoint constraints, but its relative entropy generally differs from the lifted relative entropy by a nonnegative conditional label-information gap. This gap reveals a path-space obstruction: the lifted optimizer cannot, in general, be identified with the direct unlabeled Schrödinger bridge after projection. We also derive the posterior-averaged Markov drift associated with the projected marginal flow, prove a kinetic-energy upper bound, and identify a common path-potential condition under which the projection gap vanishes. Several numerical illustrations showing density and shape control are recorded for a self-contained exposition.

</details>


### [118] [Random-Subspace Frank--Wolfe over Strongly Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24819)
*Pierre-Louis Poirion,Sebastian Pokutta,Akiko Takeda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出随机子空间Frank-Wolfe（RSFW）框架，通过在随机低维仿射截面上执行线性最小化预言（LMO）替代全空间LMO，在保持可行性的同时显著降低计算开销。在强凸可行集上证明了O(1/k)收敛率和高概率/几乎必然收敛性，以及线性和有限和随机梯度下的收敛理论，并展示了随机截面可改善局部曲率模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Frank-Wolfe方法避免投影，但在曲率较大的可行区域中，全空间线性最小化预言（LMO）本身成为计算瓶颈。现有方法缺乏在保持可行性的前提下，通过随机化降低LMO计算成本的通用框架。

Method: 提出随机子空间Frank-Wolfe（RSFW），在每次迭代中随机选取低维仿射截面（子空间），在该截面上执行精确LMO，同时通过适当步长确保迭代点始终位于原可行集内。利用强凸可行集的几何性质建立近似预言不等式，并分析压缩Hessian对局部曲率的影响。

Result: 1）对光滑凸目标函数和紧致强凸可行集，证明O(1/k)开环收敛率（高概率/几乎必然）；2）在短步长和梯度下界条件下证明线性收敛；3）将次线性理论推广至有限和随机梯度情形；4）证明随机截面下的局部曲率由压缩Hessian控制，对球和椭球上的二次目标可计算d×d曲率常数。

Conclusion: RSFW是首个在随机低维截面上替代全空间LMO的Frank-Wolfe框架，为投影自由优化中的预言机侧随机化提供了几何理论基础，在计算效率与收敛保证之间取得了良好的平衡。

Abstract: Frank--Wolfe methods avoid projections, but over curved feasible regions the full-space linear minimization oracle (LMO) can itself become the computational bottleneck. We introduce random-subspace Frank--Wolfe (RSFW), the first Frank--Wolfe framework, to our knowledge, that replaces the ambient LMO by exact LMOs over random low-dimensional affine sections of a general feasible set, while preserving feasibility in the original space. For smooth convex objectives over compact strongly convex feasible sets, we prove a dimension-explicit approximate-oracle inequality and derive the standard \(O(1/k)\) open-loop rate, with high-probability and almost-sure counterparts. Under short steps and a gradient lower bound, the same geometric control yields linear convergence, and we extend the sublinear theory to finite-sum stochastic gradients.
