We also show that random sections can improve the local curvature model controlling short steps: for smooth objectives, the quadratic model along a sampled section is governed by the compressed Hessian, yielding computable \(d\times d\) curvature constants for quadratic objectives over balls and ellipsoids. These results provide a geometric theory of oracle-side randomization in projection-free optimization.

</details>


### [119] [An Infeasible Method with Feasibility Safeguard for Nonsmooth Composite Optimization Over Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.24954)
*Xiyua Xie,Qia Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对紧致嵌入子流形上的非光滑复合优化问题，提出了一种可行性保护的近似近端线性化方法（FSIPL），允许不可行迭代但控制其有界，并证明了收敛性、复杂度及数值有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决紧致嵌入子流形上非光滑复合优化问题，现有方法难以平衡不可行迭代的灵活性与约束违反的控制，且缺乏收敛性和复杂度保证。

Method: 提出FSIPL方法，每次迭代近似求解强凸近端线性化子问题，执行校正步降低约束违反，并通过基于罚函数的非单调回溯线搜索选择步长和接受试探点。可行性保护机制同时嵌入校正和线搜索中以控制不可行性。

Result: 证明了回溯的有限终止、子序列收敛到稳定点以及$O(\varepsilon^{-2})$的外迭代复杂度；在KL假设下证明全序列收敛；数值实验（稀疏PCA、稀疏谱聚类）验证了效率。

Conclusion: FSIPL方法在保持不可行迭代灵活性的同时，通过可行性保护机制确保有界性，并提供了完整的收敛性理论和复杂度保证，在非光滑流形优化问题上具有理论价值和实践效果。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider nonsmooth composite optimization over compact embedded submanifolds defined by nonlinear equality constraints. We propose a feasibility-safeguarded inexact proximal linearized method (FSIPL), which allows infeasible iterates while keeping them within a prescribed bounded neighborhood of the manifold. Each iteration approximately solves a strongly convex proximal linearized subproblem, performs a correction step to reduce constraint violation, and uses a merit-function-based nonmonotone backtracking line search to select stepsizes and accept trial iterates. The feasibility safeguard, incorporated into both correction and line search, controls infeasibility and makes the boundedness needed in the analysis a consequence of the algorithmic design. We prove finite termination of backtracking, subsequential convergence to stationary points, and an $O(\varepsilon^{-2})$ outer iteration complexity bound. Under a Kurdyka--Łojasiewicz assumption on a suitable auxiliary function, we further establish full-sequence convergence. Numerical results on sparse PCA and sparse spectral clustering illustrate its efficiency.

</details>


### [120] [A note on the complexity of two-stage stochastic linear optimization with small second stage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25028)
*Christoph Buchheim*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了二阶段随机线性优化的计算复杂性，证明该问题在强#P-难意义下是困难的，即使随机变量是一维均匀分布、第二阶段约束数量固定，或者第二阶段变量数量固定时，困难性仍然存在。只有当随机向量维度和第二阶段约束数量同时固定时，问题才变得可解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知二阶段随机线性优化在随机变量独立均匀分布且具有固定补偿时是#P-难的，作者希望进一步刻画该问题的精确复杂性边界，探究不同参数固定下问题的难解程度。

Method: 通过将问题归约到计算二维多面体面积（由紧扩展形式给出），证明该问题在强#P-难意义下的困难性；同时分别考察随机向量维度、第二阶段约束数量和第二阶段变量数量固定时的复杂性变化。

Result: (1) 二阶段随机线性优化是强#P-难的，即使随机变量是一维均匀分布；(2) 固定第二阶段约束数量时问题仍是强#P-难的；(3) 固定第二阶段变量数量时问题是弱#P-难的；(4) 同时固定随机向量维度和第二阶段约束数量时，问题可解。

Conclusion: 二阶段随机线性优化的计算复杂性具有精细的分层结构：不同参数组合下问题的困难程度不同，只有在最严格的参数限制下才能实现可解性。

Abstract: Two-stage stochastic linear optimization is known to be #P-hard when all involved random variables are independently and uniformly distributed over intervals, even with fixed recourse. We show that this problem is actually #P-hard in the strong sense. More surprisingly, this hardness persists when the random vector is one-dimensional, i.e., uniformly distributed over a single interval. To obtain this result, we show that computing the area of a two-dimensional polytope given by a compact extended formulation is strongly #P-hard. Furthermore, we obtain the same complexity result in case the number of second-stage constraints is fixed (for a problem in standard form), while fixing the number of second-stage variables leads to a weakly #P-hard problem. Finally, if both the dimension of the random vector and the number of second-stage constraints are fixed, the problem turns out to be tractable.

</details>


### [121] [Safe Trajectory Tracking of the Stefan Problem with Second-Order Moving Boundary Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25084)
*Shumon Koga,Miroslav Krstic*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对具有二阶移动边界动态的Stefan问题，提出了一种安全的轨迹跟踪方法。通过级数展开设计前馈控制解决逆Stefan问题，基于能量成型推导轨迹跟踪控制器确保模型约束安全，并利用PDE反推变换和Lyapunov分析证明闭环系统全局指数稳定，数值仿真验证了正弦参考轨迹下的有效跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决具有二阶移动边界动态的Stefan问题中的安全轨迹跟踪问题，确保闭环系统中模型约束的安全性。

Method: 采用级数展开方法设计前馈控制解决逆Stefan问题；基于能量成型方法推导轨迹跟踪控制器；通过PDE反推变换和Lyapunov分析进行稳定性证明。

Result: 闭环系统全局指数稳定，数值仿真显示在正弦参考轨迹下具有良好的跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效实现Stefan问题的安全轨迹跟踪，且代码已开源。

Abstract: This paper considers a safe trajectory tracking of the Stefan problem with a second-order moving boundary dynamics. The model is given by a parabolic Partial Differential Equation (PDE) defined on a time-varying domain of moving boundary governed by a second-order Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) associated with the Neumann boundary condition. A feedforward control is designed by a series expansion approach to solve the inverse Stefan problem under given reference trajectory of the moving boundary, and the convergence of infinite series is proven. A trajectory tracking controller is derived based on an energy-shaping, which ensures the safety of the model constraint in the closed-loop system. The closed-loop system is also shown to be globally exponentially stable with respect to the tracking error by performing PDE backstepping transformation and Lyapunov analysis. Numerical simulation illustrates an effective tracking performance of the proposed method under a sinusoidal reference trajectory. Code is released at https://github.com/shumon0423/StefanTracking_ACC2026.git.

</details>


### [122] [Price of Coupling in Multilevel Linear Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25100)
*Nagisa Sugishita,Margarida Carvalho*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统刻画了多层级线性规划（k-level LP）在有无链接约束、变量是否有界等不同假设下的计算复杂度。主要发现：可行性问题在$k\ge2$时为$\Sigma^{p}_{k-1}$-完全；无链接约束且变量无界时，$k\le4$可多项式求解，$k\ge5$变为$\Sigma^{p}_{k-1}$-完全。最优解存在性问题在双层时为DP-完全，$k\ge3$时为$\Delta^{p}_k$-完全。最优目标值计算对任意$k\ge2$为F$\Delta^{p}_k$-完全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多层级规划是建模层次化决策的标准框架，但其计算复杂度的精细刻画（尤其是不同建模假设的影响）尚不完整。本文旨在系统地填补这一理论空白，揭示复杂度跃升的临界条件。

Method: 通过多项式归约和多项式层次（PH）中各类复杂类（$\Sigma^{p}_{k-1}$、$\Delta^{p}_k$、DP、F$\Delta^{p}_k$）的完备性证明，分别针对可行解存在性、最优解存在性、最优目标值计算三类问题，在有无链接约束、变量是否有界等组合条件下进行复杂度分类分析。

Result: (1) 可行性：$k\ge2$时$\Sigma^{p}_{k-1}$-完全；无链接约束且无界变量时，$k\le4$可多项式求解，$k\ge5$跳升至$\Sigma^{p}_{k-1}$-完全。(2) 最优解存在性：双层为DP-完全；$k\ge3$时为$\Delta^{p}_k$-完全；无链接约束且无界变量时$k=2,3$可多项式求解，$k\ge4$仍为$\Delta^{p}_k$-完全。(3) 最优目标值：任意$k\ge2$为F$\Delta^{p}_k$-完全。

Conclusion: 多层级线性规划的复杂度在$k=4$到$k=5$之间存在剧烈跃升（假设多项式层次不 collapse）；有无链接约束对复杂度的根本性影响被量化刻画；不存在多项式时间Turing机可将带链接约束的双层LP实例无损失地转化为无链接约束形式（除非P=NP），但对$k\ge5$则存在这样的机器。

Abstract: Multilevel programming is the standard framework for modeling hierarchical decision-making. In this paper, we characterize the computational complexity of deciding the existence of feasible and optimal solutions, as well as computing the optimal objective value in multilevel linear programming (LP). Our analysis considers various combinations of modeling assumptions, including the presence or absence of linking (coupling) constraints and whether all variables are bounded.
  In particular, we show the feasibility problem of $k$-level LP is $Σ^{p}_{k-1}$-complete for $k \ge 2$. Without linking constraints and unbounded variables, it is polynomial-time solvable for $k \le 4$ but becomes $Σ^{p}_{k-1}$-complete for $k \ge 5$, indicating a sharp jump in computational complexity assuming the polynomial hierarchy does not collapse. Combined with other results, one major implication is that no polynomial-time Turing machine can transform a bilevel LP instance with linking constraints into one without linking constraints while preserving feasibility unless P $=$ NP. In contrast, such machines exist for all $k \ge 5$.
  We observe similar phenomena with the decision of the existence of an optimal solution. In the bilevel case, feasibility and boundedness fully characterize the existence of an optimal solution, implying that the problem is DP-complete. However, these conditions are insufficient for $k \ge 3$ and the problem for $k \ge 3$ is $Δ^{p}_k$-complete. Similar to the feasibility problem, the problem becomes polynomially solvable for $k=2,3$ without linking constraints and unbounded variables. However, the problem is $Δ^{p}_k$-complete for $k \ge 4$, even with these simplifying assumptions. The computation of the optimal objective value is F$Δ^{p}_k$-complete for any $k \ge 2$, even without linking constraints and unbounded variables.

</details>


### [123] [Boosted Stochastic Frank-Wolfe for Constrained Nonconvex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25255)
*Navil Nandhan,Abbas Khademi,Antonio Silveti-Falls*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种不依赖梯度Lipschitz常数的步长策略，将Boosted Frank-Wolfe算法推广到随机设置，支持多种梯度估计器（SAGA、L-SVRG、SAG、Heavy Ball动量、零阶估计器等），并在非凸和拟凸目标上首次建立了收敛率。实验表明该算法在稀疏逻辑回归和量子过程断层扫描中比非加速基线收敛更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Boosted Frank-Wolfe算法仅适用于确定性凸问题，且步长需依赖线搜索或梯度Lipschitz常数，限制了其在随机优化场景中的应用。

Method: 提出一种不依赖梯度Lipschitz常数的新型步长策略，将其与多种现代梯度估计器（SAGA、L-SVRG、SAG、动量、零阶估计等）结合，将Boosted Frank-Wolfe扩展至随机优化、非凸和拟凸目标。

Result: 在保留原始随机Frank-Wolfe最坏情况收敛率的前提下，首次给出了Boosted Frank-Wolfe在非凸和拟凸目标上的收敛率。实验显示在稀疏逻辑回归和量子过程断层扫描中，每梯度调用和实际时间的收敛速度均优于非加速基线。

Conclusion: 所提步长策略有效去除了对Lipschitz常数的依赖，使Boosted Frank-Wolfe能泛化到更广泛的随机和非凸场景，且保持理论保证和实际加速效果。

Abstract: The boosted Frank-Wolfe algorithm accelerates the classical Frank-Wolfe algorithm by better aligning the update direction with the negative gradient. Its analysis, however, has been limited to deterministic convex problems, with step sizes that require either line search or knowledge of the Lipschitz constant of the gradient. We develop a novel step size strategy that does not depend on the Lipschitz constant of the gradient, which allows us to extend the boosted Frank-Wolfe algorithm to the stochastic setting. We prove that boosting with this step size strategy can be combined with many modern gradient estimators, including SAGA, L-SVRG, SAG, Heavy Ball momentum, and zeroth-order estimators, among others, while retaining the worst-case convergence rates of ordinary stochastic Frank-Wolfe. Our analysis also yields the first convergence rates for boosted Frank-Wolfe on nonconvex and quasar-convex objectives, results which are new even for deterministic problems. Experiments on sparse logistic regression and quantum process tomography show that stochastic boosted Frank-Wolfe achieves faster convergence per gradient oracle call (and on wall-clock) compared to the non-boosted baseline.

</details>


### [124] [Uncertainty Quantification in Data-Driven Inverse Optimization via Bayesian Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25288)
*Timothy C. Y. Chan,Nathan Sandholtz,Nasrin Yousefi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种层次贝叶斯框架，用于数据驱动逆优化中未知参数的不确定性量化，通过两种MCMC算法估计后验分布并构建可信区域，实验证明了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有逆优化方法仅返回点估计，缺乏对估计参数不确定性的量化，而实际数据驱动场景中参数天然具有不确定性。

Method: 提出层次贝叶斯框架，针对两种数据生成过程设计两种马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛算法，估计未知参数向量的后验分布并构建可信区域。

Result: 数值实验表明可信区域具有接近名义水平的经验覆盖率，且区域大小随观测决策数量增加而收缩。

Conclusion: 在标准可识别性条件下建立了后验一致性，验证了所提框架在参数不确定性量化中的有效性。

Abstract: Inverse optimization (IO) is used to estimate unknown parameters of an optimization model from observed decisions. In the data-driven context, the estimated parameters are inherently uncertain, yet quantifying this uncertainty has received limited attention in the literature, where existing methods return a point estimate. In this paper, we propose a hierarchical Bayesian framework for parameter uncertainty quantification in data-driven inverse optimization. Considering two data-generating processes, we develop two Markov chain Monte Carlo algorithms to estimate the posterior distribution of the unknown parameter vector, which is used to construct credible regions. We establish posterior consistency under standard identifiability conditions. Numerical experiments demonstrate near-nominal empirical coverage of the credible regions and show that the regions shrink as the number of observed decisions increases.

</details>


### [125] [A Sequential Quadratic Programming Perspective on Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25318)
*Abhijeet,Suman Chakravorty*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了牛顿法、迭代线性二次调节器(iLQR)和微分动态规划(DDP)在离散时间最优控制问题中的表现，提出了统一视角，证明iLQR是一种原则性的序列二次规划(SQP)方法，总能保证成本下降方向和收敛性，而牛顿法和DDP在远离最优初始化时缺乏这些保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于牛顿法、iLQR和DDP的比较工作尚不充分，特别是对iLQR的理论性质缺乏深入理解，常被误认为是忽略海森项的DDP近似。需要提供更严格的数学分析和统一视角来阐明三者关系。

Method: 从统一视角出发，将三种方法视为求解一系列二次规划的过程。通过理论分析证明iLQR本质上是原则性的SQP方法，并利用数值实验验证理论推导。

Result: iLQR总能保证产生成本下降方向并在温和假设下收敛到最优；牛顿法和DDP在远离最优初始化时缺乏这些保证。

Conclusion: iLQR具有比牛顿法和DDP更优越的收敛保证特性，是一种更稳健的最优控制求解方法。

Abstract: This paper investigates the performance of Newton's method, iterative Linear Quadratic Regulator (iLQR), and Differential Dynamic Programming (DDP) in solving discrete-time optimal control problems. We offer a unified perspective on these approaches, centered on the understanding that each method ultimately solves a sequence of quadratic programs. Building upon previous comparative works, this paper contributes additional mathematical explanations and results to the analysis.
  In particular, it is shown that iLQR is a principled Sequential Quadratic Programming (SQP) approach, rather than merely an approximation of DDP that neglects Hessian terms. This characteristic guarantees that iLQR will always produce a cost-descent direction and converge to an optimum, under some mild assumptions. In contrast, Newton's method and DDP lack these guarantees, especially when initialized far from an optimum. A series of numerical examples are presented to corroborate the mathematical reasoning and analysis developed in the paper.

</details>


### [126] [Sparse Recovery via $\ell_p^p/\ell_q^p$ Ratio Minimization: Theory and Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25397)
*Lang Yu,Nan-jing Huang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为约束ℓₚᵖ/ℓ_qᵖ比率模型（0<p≤1, q>1）建立了统一的理论与分析框架，涵盖了确定性精确恢复条件、稳定恢复理论以及分数算法的收敛性分析，并提出了可直接处理分数结构的prox-linear Dinkelbach算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束ℓₚᵖ/ℓ_qᵖ比率模型具有尺度不变性，在稀疏信号恢复中很有吸引力，但其非凸、非光滑且带分数结构的特点使得在0<p≤1, q>1范围内缺乏统一的理论与算法分析框架。

Method: (1) 建立了两个确定性充分条件：局部最优性判据和保证一致恢复的零空间条件；对ℓ₁/ℓ_q子族，将零空间条件转化为各向同性次高斯矩阵的高概率样本复杂度界。(2) 在k稀疏假设下，改进了基于RIP的稳定恢复理论，放松了充分条件并导出更紧的重构误差界；对可压缩信号建立了RIP-ROP误差估计，常数与环境维度无关。(3) 提出了prox-linear Dinkelbach算法直接处理约束问题的分数结构并证明了收敛性。

Result: (1) 建立了统一的理论框架，覆盖了确定性精确恢复、稳定恢复和算法收敛性。(2) 改进了稀疏和可压缩信号的恢复误差界，常数不依赖环境维度。(3) 数值实验表明合适的(p,q)选择对高动态范围稀疏信号和相干感知矩阵有效。

Conclusion: 该统一框架为约束ℓₚᵖ/ℓ_qᵖ比率模型提供了完整的理论与算法支撑，并为实际应用中的参数选择提供了指导。

Abstract: The constrained $\ell_p^p/\ell_q^p$ ratio model is scale invariant and is therefore attractive for sparse signal recovery. However, its nonconvex, nonsmooth, and fractional structure makes a unified theoretical and algorithmic analysis challenging for $0<p\le1$ and $q>1$. This paper develops a unified framework for this general model, covering deterministic exact recovery, stable recovery for sparse and compressible signals, and convergence analysis of a fractional algorithm. We first establish two deterministic sufficient conditions for exact recovery: a local optimality criterion and a null-space condition ensuring uniform recovery. For the $\ell_1/\ell_q$ subfamily, this null-space condition is further converted into high-probability sample-complexity bounds for isotropic sub-Gaussian matrix. We then study noisy recovery. Under the $k$-sparsity assumption, we improve the RIP-based stable recovery theory by relaxing the required sufficient condition and deriving sharper reconstruction-error bounds. For compressible signals, we establish RIP--ROP based error estimates whose constants are independent of the ambient dimension, improving prior bounds with explicit dimension-dependent factors [1]. An RIP-only variant is also derived. On the algorithmic side, we propose a prox-linear Dinkelbach framework that directly handles the fractional structure of the constrained problem and prove its convergence. Numerical experiments demonstrate that suitable choices of $(p,q)$ are effective for high-dynamic-range sparse signals and coherent sensing matrices.

</details>


### [127] [Specular gradient methods for nonsmooth convex optimization in Euclidean spaces: a subgradient selection strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25490)
*Kiyuob Jung*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对欧氏空间中的非光滑凸优化问题，提出了基于“镜面梯度”（specular gradients）的三种次梯度方法，证明了在适当步长下的收敛性，并通过数值实验表明这些方法能有效最小化经典方法无法处理的不可微函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决经典次梯度方法在处理特定非光滑凸优化问题时效果不佳的问题，通过识别凸函数次微分中的特殊元素（镜面梯度）来改进算法性能。

Method: 首先识别凸函数次微分中的特殊元素“镜面梯度”，然后基于此提出了三种数值方法，将其嵌入次梯度方法框架中，并在合适的步长条件下证明收敛性。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的三种方法能够有效最小化经典方法无法最小化的不可微函数，证明了镜面梯度在实际优化中的有效性。

Conclusion: 镜面梯度是次微分中一类有价值的特殊元素，基于此设计的次梯度方法在非光滑凸优化中具有优于经典方法的潜力，为处理困难不可微问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: This paper deals with nonsmooth convex optimization problems in Euclidean spaces. We identify special elements of the subdifferential of a convex function, called specular gradients. Based on this observation, we propose three numerical methods that use specular gradients in subgradient methods. We prove the convergence of the proposed methods under suitable step sizes. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed methods are capable of minimizing non-differentiable functions that classical methods fail to minimize.

</details>


### [128] [Cesàro means of firmly nonexpansive iterates need not converge strongly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25491)
*Heinz H. Bauschke,Tran Thanh Tung*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在无限维希尔伯特空间中构造了新的显式反例，证明即使对于严格非扩张算子，Baillon非线性平均遍历定理也不成立，且Cesàro均值可能不收敛到不动点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 严格非扩张算子的迭代弱收敛但不保证强收敛（Genel和Lindenstrauss, 1975）。本文旨在探索平均技术（Cesàro均值）是否能改善收敛性，即检验von Neumann线性平均遍历定理能否推广到非线性严格非扩张情形。

Method: 在无限维希尔伯特空间中构造两类反例：(1) 调和情形下，迭代的Cesàro均值始终远离唯一不动点；(2) 块构造变体，使得Cesàro均值的范数下极限为零而上极限为正，呈现振荡行为。

Result: 调和情形下Cesàro均值有界但远离不动点；块构造变体下Cesàro均值范数的liminf=0而limsup>0，即不收敛到不动点。

Conclusion: von Neumann线性平均遍历定理不能推广到Baillon非线性平均遍历定理的严格非扩张算子情形，揭示了平均技术在无限维优化中的固有限制。

Abstract: Firmly nonexpansive operators arise naturally as resolvents of monotone operators and as generalizations of projections and proximal mappings in convex optimization and fixed point theory. While their iterates are known to converge weakly to a fixed point, strong convergence is not guaranteed (Genel and Lindenstrauss, 1975).
  In this paper, we provide a new explicit family of counterexamples in infinite-dimensional Hilbert spaces. In the harmonic case the Cesàro means of the iterates remain bounded away from the unique fixed point. A block-construction variant yields Cesàro means whose norms oscillate in the sense that their liminf is zero while their limsup is positive. These results show that von Neumann's classical mean ergodic theorem for linear operators does not extend to Baillon's nonlinear mean ergodic theorem even in the firmly nonexpansive setting, and they illustrate inherent limitations of averaging techniques in infinite-dimensional optimization.

</details>


### [129] [On the Strong Quasiconvexity of Norms and Distance Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25614)
*V. S. T. Long,N. M. Nam*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限维赋范空间中范数和距离函数的强拟凸性，给出了范数函数在凸集上强拟凸的充要条件，并首次系统研究了距离函数的强拟凸性，推广了文献中的若干结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然欧几里得范数在有界凸集上已知是强拟凸的，但一般范数这一性质的完整刻画仍是开放问题。本文旨在填补这一空白，并开启对距离函数强拟凸性的研究。

Method: 通过建立范数函数在凸集上强拟凸的充要条件，并运用类似的分析框架首次研究距离函数的强拟凸性。

Result: 给出了有限维赋范空间中范数函数在凸集上强拟凸的充要条件，并得到了距离函数强拟凸性的相关结论。

Conclusion: 本文为范数和距离函数的几何性质提供了新的见解，并推广了文献中的若干已有结果。

Abstract: This paper studies the strong quasiconvexity of norm and distance functions in finite-dimensional normed spaces. Although the Euclidean norm is known to be strongly quasiconvex on bounded convex sets, a complete characterization of this property for general norms remains open. We establish necessary and sufficient conditions for a norm function to be strongly quasiconvex on a convex set. We also initiate the study of the strong quasiconvexity of distance functions. Our results provide new insights into the geometric properties of norm and distance functions and extend several existing results in the literature.

</details>


### [130] [The Geometry of Linear Program Compression: An Exact Characterization and Learning Algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25635)
*Yuhan Ye,Omar Bennouna*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究线性规划（LP）的压缩问题，提出一种基于几何特征的精确压缩方法，可在完全保留最优性的前提下将LP降维，并给出样本复杂度为$1-\widetilde O(d^\star/n)$的快速学习保证，优于传统近似投影方法的$\widetilde O(1/\sqrt n)$收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动投影方法虽能学习低维近似LP并保证近似的目标值，但无法在预设压缩预算下识别最优投影。本文希望回答一个更精确的问题：在完全保持最优性的前提下，LP能被压缩到多低的维度，从而实现更快的重复求解且不损失解的质量。

Method: 给出精确压缩LP的几何特征刻画；提出基于样本的可学习算法，具有快速率保证（$1-\widetilde O(d^\star/n)$）。框架还揭示了压缩LP维度与恢复最优解概率之间的可调权衡。

Result: 压缩LP恢复未见实例最优解的概率至少为$1-\widetilde O(d^\star/n)$，其中$d^\star$是决策相关子空间的维度，$n$是历史LP样本数量。该$1/n$依赖率优于近似投影方法的$\widetilde O(1/\sqrt n)$一致收敛率。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种在精确保持最优性前提下的LP压缩理论框架与算法，允许用户在压缩程度与准确性之间进行灵活权衡。

Abstract: We study how much a linear program (LP) can be compressed when solved repeatedly, given prior knowledge about its objective function. Existing data-driven projection methods learn low-dimensional surrogate LPs with approximate objective-value guarantees, but cannot provably identify the optimal projection for a prescribed compression budget. We instead ask a sharper question: how far can an LP be compressed into a lower-dimensional equivalent while \emph{exactly} preserving optimality, enabling faster repeated solves with no loss in solution quality? We provide an exact geometric characterization of such compressed LPs, together with a tractable sample-based learning algorithm that comes with fast-rate guarantees: the compressed LP recovers the optimal solution of an unseen instance with probability at least $1-\widetilde O(d^\star/n)$, where $d^\star$ is the dimension of the decision-relevant subspace, and $n$ is the number of available historical LP samples. This $1/n$ dependence is sharper than the $\widetilde O(1/\sqrt n)$ uniform-convergence rates of approximate projection methods. Our framework further exposes a tunable tradeoff between the dimension of the compressed LP and the probability of recovering the optimal solution, allowing the user to trade compression for accuracy.

</details>


### [131] [Comments and Corrections on the DP Equations of Paper "On Team Decision Problems With Nonclassical Information Structures"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25700)
*Charalambos D. Charalambous,Umarbek Guvercin,Seddik Djouadi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文指出[1]中关于延迟共享信息模式的定理5和定理7的DP方程证明有误，导致多个相关结论失效，并给出了正确的PbP最优性DP方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠正[1]中关于非经典信息结构下团队决策问题的错误证明，揭示延迟共享信息模式分析中的微妙性和挑战。

Method: 通过分析[1]中信息状态递归和DP方程证明的逻辑缺陷，指出其错误根源，并重新推导正确的PbP最优性DP方程。

Result: 证明了[1]中定理5的证明和定理7的DP方程(57)(58)不正确，导致引理8、定理6及定理7完整陈述均无效。

Conclusion: 延迟共享信息模式的PbP最优性DP方程需要更严谨的推导，[1]中的错误源于信息状态定义和分离策略最优性证明的缺陷。

Abstract: The 2023 paper ``On Team Decision Problems With Nonclassical Information Structures'' [1] presented information states and dynamic programming (DP) equations for delayed sharing information patterns, based on the concept of person-by-person (PbP) optimality of static team theory. In particular, [Section IV, 1], Theorem 5 presents recursions of the information states and Theorem 7, eqn(57), eqn(58), presents DP equations, of each team member.
  In this note we show that the proof of Theorem 5 and the DP eqn(57), eqn(58) of Theorem 7 of [1] are incorrect. Consequently, Lemma 8, Theorem 6, and the full statement of Theorem 7 in [1] are invalid, because their proofs rely on erroneous information states and the optimality of separated strategies (i.e., functions of the information states).
  We further provide the correct DP equations for PbP optimality, thereby highlighting the subtleties and challenges inherent in the analysis of delayed sharing information patterns.

</details>


### [132] [An Anderson-accelerated stochastic extragradient method for stochastic variational inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25837)
*Xin Qu,Wei Bian,Xiaojun Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合Anderson加速的随机外梯度算法（AASE），用于求解一类随机变分不等式问题。在仅对随机算子的期望施加伪单调性假设的弱条件下，证明了算法几乎必然收敛到解，并建立了次线性收敛率和最优Oracle复杂度，数值实验验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机外梯度方法在求解随机变分不等式时，通常需要对每个随机样本算子或其样本均值施加伪单调性假设，这一条件过于严格。本文旨在放宽这一假设，使其仅作用于随机算子的期望，同时仍能保证算法的收敛性并提升收敛速度。

Method: 将Anderson加速技术引入随机外梯度方法（SEG）中，在随机逼近框架下构造AASE算法。Anderson加速度利用历史迭代信息修正当前迭代方向，以加速收敛；同时在理论分析中，仅要求随机算子的期望满足伪单调性，而对采样算子不作此限制。

Result: 证明了AASE算法生成的序列几乎必然收敛到随机变分不等式问题的解；建立了关于平均残差函数的次线性收敛率；达到了最优Oracle复杂度；数值实验验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过引入Anderson加速，在更弱的伪单调性条件下，实现了随机变分不等式问题的高效求解，在理论和实践层面均优于传统随机外梯度方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose an Anderson-accelerated stochastic extragradient algorithm for solving a class of stochastic variational inequalities, by incorporating Anderson acceleration into the stochastic extragradient method under a stochastic approximation framework. A key challenge in our setting is that the pseudomonotonicity assumption is only imposed on the expectation of the stochastic operator, rather than on the individual stochastic operator itself and the sample averages utilized in the algorithm. We prove that, despite the lack of pseudomonotonicity in the sampled operators, the sequence generated by the proposed algorithm converges almost surely to a solution of the stochastic variational inequality problem. Additionally, we establish the sublinear convergence rate of the proposed algorithm in terms of the mean residual function, along with its optimal oracle complexity. Finally, we validate the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm through numerical experiments.

</details>


### [133] [Exact controllability of the stochastic Maxwell equation: theory and numerical simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25932)
*Liying Sun,Xiaohan Wang,Yongyi Yu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究三维随机Maxwell方程的精确可控性，通过乘子法建立后向随机Maxwell方程的可观测性不等式，进而证明前向方程的精确可控性。扩散项控制不可或缺，且所用控制数量最小。最后提出结合中心差分、中点格式和拉格朗日乘子法的数值算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三维随机Maxwell方程的精确可控性，特别是确定实现精确可控所需的最小控制数量，以及扩散项控制在其中的关键作用。

Method: 采用乘子法建立后向随机Maxwell方程的可观测性不等式，进而证明前向方程的精确可控性。数值方法上，结合空间中心差分、时间中点格式和拉格朗日乘子法求解控制值。

Result: 证明了三维随机Maxwell方程的精确可控性，揭示了扩散项控制的不可或缺性，并表明该可控性是以最小数量的控制实现的。数值实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 三维随机Maxwell方程的精确可控性成立，扩散项控制是必要条件，且控制数量已达到理论最小值。所提出的数值算法有效且有助于理解理论框架。

Abstract: This article investigates the exact controllability of three-dimensional stochastic Maxwell equations, a coupled system comprising two stochastic partial differential equations. The research establishes the observability inequality for the backward stochastic Maxwell equations using the multiplier method, and subsequently, proves the exact controllability of the forward equations. The control acting on the diffusion term is found to be indispensable, since exact controllability is destroyed when this control is removed; it is further proved that the controllability result obtained in this paper is achieved with a minimal number of controls. Finally, a numerical algorithm combining a central difference for spatial discretization, a midpoint scheme for temporal discretization and Lagrange multiplier method is proposed, yielding numerical results that offer the control value and lead to deeper insights into the underlying theoretical framework.

</details>
